  }
  if(idx===-1)idx=keyframeAtPlayhead(clip,tSec);
  if(idx === -1) return false; // not parked on an existing keyframe — nothing to update
  const currentPose = {};
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(baseAngles[k] !== undefined) currentPose[k] = bones[k].angle - baseAngles[k];
  });
  clip.keyframes[idx].pose = currentPose;
  clip.keyframes[idx].transform = { ...modelTransform };
  if(!refreshCharacterKeyframeMarkers(selectedClip,clip))renderTimelineClip({skipOverlayRender:true});
  updateKeyButtonState();
  return true;
}

// CapCut-style single button: tap once to drop a keyframe at the EXACT current playhead
// position (capturing the live pose AND the current Transform — size/position/rotation —
// so two keyframes with different Transform snapshots animate the character gliding/
// resizing/rotating between them, not just its bone pose); tap again while the playhead is
// still on that same keyframe to remove it. This replaces the old always-add
// addKeyframeAtPlayhead(). Acts on whichever stickman clip is currently selected (main or
// any split/appended segment) — not just the main clip — falling back to the main clip when
// nothing stickman-related is selected (e.g. pressed straight from the transport bar).
function toggleKeyframeAtPlayhead(){
  // Keyframes only ever apply to a stickman clip's pose (main clip or a split segment) —
  // background and overlay clips are plain media with no pose to key. Selecting one of
  // those and tapping Key now says so plainly instead of silently keyframing whichever
  // stickman clip happens to be loaded underneath (that used to happen because the
  // fallback below reached for `activeClip` when getClipByRef came back empty).
  const isCharacterLayerSelection = typeof selectedClip === 'string' && /^char\d+$/.test(selectedClip);
  const isStickmanSelection = selectedClip === 'main' || (typeof selectedClip === 'string' && selectedClip.startsWith('seg')) || isCharacterLayerSelection;
  if(!isStickmanSelection){
    if(ethvelleObjectClipById(selectedClip)){toggleObjectKeyframeAtPlayhead();return;}
    showToast('This layer has no keyframes to set', {icon: toastIcons.info});
    return;
  }
  if(!activeClip){
    showToast('Pick an animation first', {icon: toastIcons.info});
    return;
  }
  // BUGFIX (keyframe-add froze the animation): this used to unconditionally call
  // pausePlayback() every time, even while Play was actively running — so dropping a
  // keyframe mid-playback always looked like "adding a keyframe freezes the character",
  // when really it was just pause firing on every tap. Keyframes are allowed to be
  // added live now: while playing, read the exact instant from the real clock
  // (getCurrentAbsoluteTime(), which derives from performance.now() even mid-playback)
  // WITHOUT stopping the rAF loop, so Play keeps running uninterrupted through the tap.
  const clip = getClipByRef(selectedClip) || activeClip;
  if(!onClipSelected(clip,'keyframe'))return;
  const tSec = getCurrentAbsoluteTime();
  const existingIdx = keyframeAtPlayhead(clip, tSec);
  if(existingIdx > -1){
    clip.keyframes.splice(existingIdx, 1);
    selectedKfIdx = null;
    if(!refreshCharacterKeyframeMarkers(selectedClip,clip))renderTimelineClip({skipOverlayRender:true});
    syncBottomToolsMode();
    updateKeyButtonState();
    showToast('Keyframe removed', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
    persistProjectState();
    return;
  }
  const currentPose = {};
  Object.keys(bones).forEach(k=>{
    if(baseAngles[k] !== undefined) currentPose[k] = bones[k].angle - baseAngles[k];
  });
  const currentTransform = { ...modelTransform };
  // remove any existing keyframe very close to this time, replace it
  clip.keyframes = clip.keyframes.filter(kf => Math.abs(kf.t - tSec) > keyframeContactWindow());
  clip.keyframes.push({ t: tSec, pose: currentPose, transform: currentTransform });
  clip.keyframes.sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t);
  selectedKfIdx=clip.keyframes.findIndex(kf=>Math.abs(Number(kf.t)-tSec)<=keyframeContactWindow());
  if(!refreshCharacterKeyframeMarkers(selectedClip,clip))renderTimelineClip({skipOverlayRender:true});
  syncBottomToolsMode();
  updateKeyButtonState();
  showToast('Keyframe added', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
  persistProjectState();
}
// Keeps both key buttons (transport row + bottom clip toolbar) in sync with whether the
// playhead is currently sitting exactly on a keyframe of whichever stickman clip is
// selected (falls back to the main clip) — CapCut fills the key icon solid when parked on one.
function updateKeyButtonState(){
  const transportBtn=document.getElementById('transportKeyBtn');
  const toolBtn=document.getElementById('clipKeyTool');
  const isCharacterLayerSelection=typeof selectedClip==='string'&&/^char\d+$/.test(selectedClip);
  const isStickmanSelection=selectedClip==='main'||(typeof selectedClip==='string'&&selectedClip.startsWith('seg'))||isCharacterLayerSelection;
  const charClip=isStickmanSelection?(getClipByRef(selectedClip)||activeClip):null;
  const objectClip=isStickmanSelection?null:ethvelleObjectClipById(selectedClip);
  const objectProperty=objectClip?ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(objectClip):null;
  const tSec=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
  const charIdx=charClip?keyframeAtPlayhead(charClip,tSec):-1;
  const objectIdx=objectClip&&Array.isArray(objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)
    ?objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.findIndex(k=>k&&k.property===objectProperty&&Math.abs(Number(k.t)-tSec)<=ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow()):-1;
  // Scrubbing onto an authored keyframe selects that existing key automatically. Leaving
  // it clears the playhead-driven selection; no marker is added or moved by scrubbing.
  if(charClip)selectedKfIdx=charIdx>-1?charIdx:null;
  if(objectClip){
    if(objectIdx>-1)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id:String(selectedClip),index:objectIdx,source:'playhead'};
    else if(window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe&&String(window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe.id)===String(selectedClip))window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
  }
  const onKey=charClip?charIdx>-1:objectIdx>-1;
  const hasTarget=!!(charClip||objectClip),badgeGlyph=onKey?'\u2212':'+';
  if(transportBtn){
    transportBtn.classList.toggle('on-key',onKey);transportBtn.classList.toggle('disabled',!hasTarget);
    const b=document.getElementById('transportKeyBadge');if(b)b.textContent=badgeGlyph;
  }
  if(toolBtn){
    toolBtn.classList.toggle('on-key',onKey);toolBtn.classList.toggle('disabled',!hasTarget);
    const b=document.getElementById('clipKeyBadge');if(b)b.textContent=badgeGlyph;
  }

  // Only touch the active layer's own marker rail. The previous implementation walked
  // every keyframe in every row on every renderFrame() call (including playback and scrub),
  // which became a large source of lag as projects accumulated clips.
  let track=null;
  if(charClip){
    const trackId=(selectedClip==='main'||isCharacterLayerSelection)?'kfTrack':'kfTrack-'+selectedClip;
    track=document.getElementById(trackId);
  }else if(objectClip){
    const cached=window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack;
    if(cached&&cached.isConnected&&String(cached.dataset.objectKfTrack||'')===String(selectedClip))track=cached;
    else{
      // A render may replace the selected row's DOM node. Recover only that one marker rail
      // by ID instead of falling back to a scan of every keyframe track.
      const id=String(selectedClip);
      track=document.querySelector(`[data-object-kf-track="${id}"]`);
      if(track)window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack=track;
    }
  }
  if(track){
    const previousMarker=window.__ethvelleUnderPlayheadMarker;
    if(previousMarker&&previousMarker.isConnected&&previousMarker.classList.contains('under-playhead'))previousMarker.classList.remove('under-playhead');
    let nextMarker=null;
    if(charClip&&charIdx>-1)nextMarker=track.querySelector(`[data-kf-idx=\"${charIdx}\"]`);
    else if(objectClip&&objectIdx>-1)nextMarker=track.querySelector(`[data-object-kf-idx=\"${objectIdx}\"]`);
    if(nextMarker)nextMarker.classList.add('under-playhead');
    // Keep the visible diamond's selected styling in sync with the playhead.
    if(charClip){
      track.querySelectorAll('[data-kf-idx]').forEach(marker=>marker.classList.toggle('active',Number(marker.dataset.kfIdx)===charIdx));
    }else if(objectClip){
      track.querySelectorAll('[data-object-kf-idx]').forEach(marker=>marker.classList.toggle('active',Number(marker.dataset.objectKfIdx)===objectIdx));
    }
    window.__ethvelleUnderPlayheadMarker=nextMarker;
  }else{
    window.__ethvelleUnderPlayheadMarker=null;
  }
}
// ref is the clip's selection ref ('main' or 'segN'); defaults to 'main' so any older
// onclick markup without a ref still behaves exactly as before.
function selectKeyframe(idx, ref){
  ref = ref || 'main';
  const clip = getClipByRef(ref);
  if(!clip) return;
  // Same as selectClipForChrome: pause first, at exactly the instant that was on screen, so
  // tapping a keyframe diamond mid-playback doesn't fight the still-running playback loop —
  // this is what made the pause/play tap feel broken right after picking a keyframe.
  pausePlayback();
  selectedKfIdx = idx;
  selectedClip = ref; // a keyframe selection implies its parent clip is the active context
  document.querySelectorAll('.kf-marker.active').forEach(m=>m.classList.remove('active'));
  const trackId = ref.startsWith('seg') ? ('kfTrack-'+ref) : 'kfTrack';
  const el = document.querySelector(`#${trackId} [data-kf-idx="${idx}"]`);
  if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  syncBottomToolsMode();
  // Seek playhead exactly to that keyframe's own stored absolute time (kf.t is ALWAYS
  // absolute timeline seconds — see PART 14) via the canonical clock. Never derive this
  // from activeClip.trimStart: `ref` may be a split segment, whose correct offset is its
  // OWN trimStart, not activeClip's — that mismatch is exactly what PART 18 flags.
  const kf = clip.keyframes[idx];
  if(kf) seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds(kf.t);
}
function deleteSelectedKeyframe(){
  if(selectedKfIdx===null) return;
  const clip = getClipByRef(selectedClip) || activeClip;
  if(!clip) return;
  clip.keyframes.splice(selectedKfIdx, 1);
  selectedKfIdx = null;
  renderTimelineClip();
  syncBottomToolsMode();
  updateKeyButtonState();
  showToast('Keyframe deleted', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
  persistProjectState();
}

/* ---------------- Model transform (size / position / rotation) ---------------- */
// BUGFIX ("made I can Transform any Background or clips position, size etc by adjust button
// when I select background or clips" — the Transform panel/button did nothing for either):
// this whole section used to only ever read/write the single shared `modelTransform` (the
// active character's own size/position/rotation) no matter what was selected — Background
// already HAD its own transform data (bgTransform, used for pinch/pan on the canvas) and
// image/video Clips got one added above (ov.transform), but neither was ever wired to the
// Transform panel's sliders, so selecting either and opening Transform silently just showed/
// edited the character's transform regardless. transformTarget() below resolves the correct
// live transform object from whatever selectedClip currently is, and every function in this
// section now reads/writes THROUGH that resolved target instead of the hardcoded
// modelTransform — so the same panel now edits the right thing no matter which row is
// selected, with the old character-editing behavior kept as the default/fallback.
// FEATURE (multiple sequential backgrounds): selectedClip for a background clip is now its
// own id ('bg'+n, mirroring how each overlay clip has its own id) instead of the single
// literal string 'background', since there can be several background clips now and each
// needs its own independent transform/selection/delete/duplicate. transformTarget resolves
// the SPECIFIC clip's own transform object, not just whichever one currently plays.
function backgroundClipForSelection(){
  if(typeof selectedClip !== 'string' || !selectedClip.startsWith('bg')) return null;
  return backgrounds.find(b=>b.id===selectedClip) || null;
}
function transformTarget(){
  const bgClip = backgroundClipForSelection();
  if(bgClip){
    if(!bgClip.transform) bgClip.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
    return { obj: bgClip.transform, kind:'background', bgClip };
  }
  if(typeof selectedClip === 'string'){
    const ov = overlays.find(o=>o.id===selectedClip);
    // Older saved/imported visual clips may predate per-clip transform data. Create it
    // lazily so the Transform panel never falls back to editing the character by mistake.
    if(ov && (ov.type==='image'||ov.type==='video')){
      if(!ov.transform) ov.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
      return { obj: ov.transform, kind:'overlay', ov };
    }
    if(ov && ov.transform) return { obj: ov.transform, kind:'overlay', ov };
  }
  // Default: the active character's own model transform — main clip, split segments, a
  // character row, or nothing selected at all all fall back to posing/sizing the stickman,
  // matching every bit of behavior this panel already had before Background/Clips existed.
  return { obj: modelTransform, kind:'character' };
}
function updateTransform(){
  const size = +document.getElementById('sizeSlider').value;
  const px = +document.getElementById('posXSlider').value;
  const py = +document.getElementById('posYSlider').value;
  const rot = +document.getElementById('rotSlider').value;
  const { obj: t, kind, ov } = transformTarget();
  t.scale = size/100;
  t.offsetX = (px/100) * (stageW*0.75);
  t.offsetY = (py/100) * (stageH*0.75);
  t.rotation = rot;
  document.getElementById('sizeVal').textContent = size+'%';
  document.getElementById('posXVal').textContent = px;
  document.getElementById('posYVal').textContent = py;
  document.getElementById('rotVal').textContent = rot+'°';
  if(kind==='background'){
    // Only visibly repaints if the EDITED clip also happens to be the one currently under
    // the playhead (see currentBackgroundClip) — editing a background clip that isn't
    // showing right now correctly has no visible effect until playback reaches it.
    refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();
    applyBgTransform();
  } else if(kind==='overlay'){
    renderFrame(); // repaints overlayMediaLayer with the clip's updated transform
  } else {
    drawStick();
    // If the playhead is parked exactly on an existing keyframe, editing Transform here must
    // update that keyframe's stored snapshot too — otherwise the model visibly moves while
    // the stored keyframe silently goes stale (PART 17). Only meaningful for the character
    // (Background/Clips have no keyframe timeline of their own).
    const updatedKeyframe = updateCurrentKeyframeFromEditorState();
    if(!updatedKeyframe && activeCharacter()) activeCharacter().restTransform = { ...modelTransform };
    const ch=activeCharacter();
    if(ch&&typeof window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource==='function'){
      const key=ch.id!=null?String(ch.id):'index:'+String(activeCharIdx);
      window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource('char:'+key,typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec);
    }
  }
  debouncedPersist();
}
let persistDebounceTimer=null;
function debouncedPersist(){
  clearTimeout(persistDebounceTimer);
  persistDebounceTimer = setTimeout(persistProjectState, 400);
}
function syncTransformSliders(){
  const sizeSlider = document.getElementById('sizeSlider');
  const posXSlider = document.getElementById('posXSlider');
  const posYSlider = document.getElementById('posYSlider');
  const rotSlider = document.getElementById('rotSlider');
  if(!sizeSlider) return;
  const { obj: t } = transformTarget();
  sizeSlider.value = Math.round(t.scale*100);
  posXSlider.value = Math.round((t.offsetX/(stageW*0.75))*100) || 0;
  posYSlider.value = Math.round((t.offsetY/(stageH*0.75))*100) || 0;
  rotSlider.value = t.rotation||0;
  document.getElementById('sizeVal').textContent = sizeSlider.value+'%';
  document.getElementById('posXVal').textContent = posXSlider.value;
  document.getElementById('posYVal').textContent = posYSlider.value;
  document.getElementById('rotVal').textContent = rotSlider.value+'°';
}
function resetTransform(){
  const { obj: t, kind } = transformTarget();
  t.scale = 1; t.offsetX = 0; t.offsetY = 0; t.rotation = 0;
  syncTransformSliders();
  if(kind==='background'){ refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead(); applyBgTransform(); }
  else if(kind==='overlay') renderFrame();
  else {
    const updatedKeyframe = updateCurrentKeyframeFromEditorState();
    if(!updatedKeyframe && activeCharacter()) activeCharacter().restTransform = { ...modelTransform };
    drawStick();
    const ch=activeCharacter();
    if(ch&&typeof window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource==='function'){
      const key=ch.id!=null?String(ch.id):'index:'+String(activeCharIdx);
      window.ethvelleRefreshTrackingFollowersForSource('char:'+key,typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec);
    }
  }
  showToast('Transform reset', {icon: toastIcons.check, duration:1200});
  persistProjectState();
}

/* ================= EXPORT (real video file download) ================= */
let exportQuality = 720;
// Export-specific controls; the editor's internal animation clock remains 24 fps.
window.exportFrameRate = 30;
window.exportBitrateMode = 'recommended';
window.exportBitrateMbps = 0; // Optional custom export bitrate in Mbps; set by the export slider.
function openExportSheet(){
  if(isExporting){
    showToast('Export already in progress', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:1400});
    return;
  }
  // Exporting is the one action that requires a signed-in account — everything else
  // (creating/editing projects) works as a guest. requireLoginThen() sends the user to the
  // login screen and automatically re-opens this same sheet the moment they sign in.
  requireLoginThen(function(){
    pausePlayback();
    renderExportQualityOptions();
    document.getElementById('exportModalBackdrop').classList.add('active');
  });
}
function closeExportSheet(){
  document.getElementById('exportModalBackdrop').classList.remove('active');
}
const QUALITY_LOCK_SVG = '<svg class="quality-lock" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="9" rx="2"/><path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 018 0v3"/></svg>';
// Greys out / locks any quality tier above what the user's current plan actually allows
// (plan.maxQuality, set by the admin's "Actual limits" controls), and makes sure the
// pre-selected tile is never a locked one. Re-run every time the sheet opens so a plan
// upgrade (or admin change) is reflected immediately.
function renderExportQualityOptions(){
  const plan = getMyPlanData();
  const maxQ = Number(plan.maxQuality) || 720;
  const opts = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#exportQualityGrid .ratio-opt'));
  let highestAllowed = null;
  opts.forEach(o=>{
    const q = Number(o.dataset.quality);
    const locked = q > maxQ;
    o.classList.toggle('locked', locked);
    // Remove any lock badge left over from a previous render before possibly re-adding one.
    const existingLock = o.querySelector('.quality-lock');
    if(existingLock) existingLock.remove();
    if(locked) o.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', QUALITY_LOCK_SVG);
    if(!locked && (highestAllowed===null || q>highestAllowed)) highestAllowed = q;
  });
  // If nothing is selected, or the currently-selected tile just became locked (e.g. the
  // admin lowered this plan's limit while the sheet was closed), fall back to the highest
  // quality this plan still allows.
  let selected = opts.find(o=>o.classList.contains('selected'));
  if(!selected || selected.classList.contains('locked')){
    opts.forEach(o=>o.classList.remove('selected'));
    const fallback = opts.find(o=>Number(o.dataset.quality)===highestAllowed) || opts[0];
    if(fallback){ fallback.classList.add('selected'); exportQuality = Number(fallback.dataset.quality); }
  }
  const hint = document.getElementById('exportQualityHint');
  const anyLocked = opts.some(o=>o.classList.contains('locked'));
  const usedExports = getExportsUsedThisMonth();
  const exportCapHit = !plan.unlimitedExports && plan.maxExports != null && usedExports >= plan.maxExports;
  if(exportCapHit){
    hint.style.display = 'block';
    hint.innerHTML = 'You\u2019ve used all <b>' + plan.maxExports + '</b> exports this month \u2014 upgrade for more.';
  } else if(anyLocked){
    hint.style.display = 'block';
    hint.innerHTML = 'Higher quality needs a bigger plan — <b>upgrade</b> to unlock it.';
  } else {
    hint.style.display = 'none';
  }
  renderExportCreditEstimate();
}
function renderExportCreditEstimate(){
  const el=document.getElementById('exportCreditEstimate');if(!el)return;
  const settings=getCreditSettings();
  if(!settings.enabled){el.textContent='Credit charging is currently disabled.';return;}
  const duration=getExportContentDurationSec();
  const cost=calculateExportCreditCost(exportQuality,duration);
  const rate=getExportCreditRate(exportQuality);
  const label=exportQuality===2160?'4K':exportQuality+'p';
  el.textContent='Estimated cost: '+cost.toLocaleString()+' credits · '+label+' · '+Math.ceil(duration)+' sec video ('+rate+' credits/min). Balance: '+(currentUser?myCreditsBalance.toLocaleString():'sign in required')+'.';
  const btn=document.getElementById('exportConfirmBtn');
  if(btn){const insufficient=currentUser&&myCreditsBalance<cost;btn.disabled=!!insufficient;btn.style.opacity=insufficient?'.62':'';}
}
function selectExportQuality(el){
  if(el.classList.contains('locked')){
    const plan = getMyPlanData();
    showToast('Upgrade your plan to export at ' + el.querySelector('.ratio-label').textContent, {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:2200});
    return;
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('#exportQualityGrid .ratio-opt').forEach(o=>o.classList.remove('selected'));
  el.classList.add('selected');
  exportQuality = +el.dataset.quality;
  renderExportCreditEstimate();
}

function qualityToHeight(q){
  // width/height derived from the project ratio, scaled so the shorter/relevant side matches q
  const [rw, rh] = (currentProject?.ratio || '9:16').split(':').map(Number);
  if(rw >= rh){
    // landscape-ish: q is the height
    const h = q, w = Math.round(q * (rw/rh));
    return {w, h};
  } else {
    // portrait: q is the width for consistent quality across ratios
    const w = q, h = Math.round(q * (rh/rw));
    return {w, h};
  }
}

let isExporting = false;
// FEATURE (extract audio / control clip volume — export mixing): builds the FULL audio mix
// for the project as a single decoded AudioBuffer, covering every video and audio overlay
// clip that carries sound, each one gain-adjusted by its own `volume` (0–2) and placed at
// its correct absolute-timeline start, trimmed and speed-adjusted exactly the way
// renderOverlayMedia/renderAudioOverlayMedia already do for live preview. The WebCodecs
// path encodes this buffer using sample-derived timestamps; only the legacy MediaRecorder
// compatibility fallback plays it through a MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode.
// Returns null if the project has no audio-bearing clips at all.
async function mixProjectAudioForExport(durationSec){
  const soundClips = overlays.filter(o=> (o.type==='video'||o.type==='audio') && o.src && (o.volume!==0 || (Array.isArray(o.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)&&o.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.some(k=>k&&k.property==='volume'&&Number(k.value)>0))));
  if(soundClips.length === 0) return null;
  const SR = 44100;
  const offlineCtx = new OfflineAudioContext(2, Math.ceil(durationSec*SR), SR);
  let anyDecoded = false;
  const audioDecodeFailures=[];
  for(const ov of soundClips){
    try{
      const resp = await fetch(ov.src);
      const arrBuf = await resp.arrayBuffer();
      // decodeAudioData's older callback form is used instead of the promise form for
      // Safari compatibility (older WebKit only supports the callback signature) — the
      // surrounding Promise wrapper gives both forms the same async/await call site.
      let audioBuf = await new Promise((resolve, reject)=> offlineCtx.decodeAudioData(arrBuf, resolve, reject));
      const audioCfg = (ov.type==='audio' && typeof getEthvelleAudioSettings==='function') ? getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov) : {voice:'natural',effect:'none',pitch:0,enhance:0,enhanceEnabled:false,noiseReduction:false,noiseStrength:65};
      // PCM enhancement/noise reduction is baked into the selected WAV after Apply.
      // Do not run its matching realtime/export stage a second time on that processed source.
      const processedModes=ov.audioProcessedModes||{};
      const noiseLive=!!audioCfg.noiseReduction&&!processedModes.noise;
      const enhanceLive=!!audioCfg.enhanceEnabled&&!processedModes.enhance;
      if(ov.type==='audio' && noiseLive && typeof window.ethvelleApplyOfflineNoiseGate==='function'){
        audioBuf = window.ethvelleApplyOfflineNoiseGate(audioBuf,audioCfg.noiseStrength);
      }
      const baseSpeed = ov.speed || 1;
      const speed = baseSpeed * Math.pow(2, Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,Number(audioCfg.pitch)||0))/12);
      const vol = ov.volume==null ? 1 : ov.volume; // Volume boost remains independent of the voice-processing controls.
      const srcNode = offlineCtx.createBufferSource();
      const exportPitchRatio = Math.pow(2, Math.max(-12,Math.min(12,Number(audioCfg.pitch)||0))/12);
      /* v221: buffer + playbackRate are assigned right before start() so clip speed can be applied pitch-safe. */
      const gainNode = offlineCtx.createGain(); gainNode.gain.value = vol;
      let exportFadeNode = null;
      if(ov.type==='audio'){
        // Standalone audio clips receive the selected voice/effect/noise processing.
        const cfg = (typeof window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile==='function') ? window.ethvelleAudioVoiceProfile(audioCfg.voice) : {highpass:20,lowpass:20000,type:'peaking',frequency:1200,q:.5,gain:0};
        const noiseAmount=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(audioCfg.noiseStrength)||0))/100;
        const highpass = offlineCtx.createBiquadFilter(); highpass.type='highpass'; highpass.frequency.value=noiseLive?80+noiseAmount*120:cfg.highpass;
        const voiceFilter = offlineCtx.createBiquadFilter(); voiceFilter.type=cfg.type; voiceFilter.frequency.value=cfg.frequency; voiceFilter.Q.value=cfg.q; voiceFilter.gain.value=cfg.gain;
        const lowpass = offlineCtx.createBiquadFilter(); lowpass.type='lowpass'; lowpass.frequency.value=noiseLive?Math.max(6500,14500-noiseAmount*4500):cfg.lowpass;
        const compressor = offlineCtx.createDynamicsCompressor();
        compressor.threshold.value=noiseLive?-38:(audioCfg.effect==='studio'?-24:(enhanceLive?-25:0));
        compressor.knee.value=audioCfg.effect==='studio'?18:24;
        compressor.ratio.value=noiseLive?4:(audioCfg.effect==='studio'?5:(enhanceLive?3.5:1));
        compressor.attack.value=.008; compressor.release.value=.18;
        const makeup = offlineCtx.createGain(); makeup.gain.value=enhanceLive ? 1+Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(audioCfg.enhance)||0))/100*.55 : 1;
        const panner = (typeof offlineCtx.createStereoPanner==='function') ? offlineCtx.createStereoPanner() : null;
        const fadeNode = offlineCtx.createGain();
        if(panner){panner.pan.value=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(Number(audioCfg.pan)||0)/100));}
        srcNode.connect(highpass); highpass.connect(voiceFilter); voiceFilter.connect(lowpass); lowpass.connect(compressor); compressor.connect(makeup);
        if(panner){makeup.connect(panner);panner.connect(fadeNode);}else{makeup.connect(fadeNode);}
        fadeNode.connect(gainNode);
        if(audioCfg.effect==='echo'){
          const delay=offlineCtx.createDelay(1); delay.delayTime.value=.24;
          const echoGain=offlineCtx.createGain(); echoGain.gain.value=.22;
          const feedback=offlineCtx.createGain(); feedback.gain.value=.22;
          srcNode.connect(delay); delay.connect(echoGain); if(panner)echoGain.connect(panner);else echoGain.connect(fadeNode); delay.connect(feedback); feedback.connect(delay);
        }
        exportFadeNode=fadeNode;
      }else{
        // Audio embedded in a video is kept unchanged; only its existing clip volume applies.
        srcNode.connect(gainNode);
      }
      gainNode.connect(offlineCtx.destination);
      // trimStart/trimEnd are absolute timeline seconds; sourceOffset is the exact source
      // second where this segment begins. Split halves therefore retain their source offset.
      const sourceOffset = Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0);
      const clipLenSourceSec = Math.max(0, (ov.trimEnd - ov.trimStart) * speed);
      if(clipLenSourceSec <= 0 || ov.trimStart >= durationSec || sourceOffset >= audioBuf.duration) continue;
      const when = Math.max(0, ov.trimStart);
      const availableSourceSec = Math.max(0,audioBuf.duration-sourceOffset);
      const outputRoomSourceSec = Math.max(0,durationSec-when)*speed;
      const playLen = Math.min(clipLenSourceSec,availableSourceSec,outputRoomSourceSec);
      if(playLen <= 0) continue;
      if(Array.isArray(ov.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)&&ov.ethvelleMediaKeyframes.some(k=>k&&k.property==='volume')){
        ethvelleScheduleObjectVolumeAutomation(gainNode.gain,ov,when,Math.min(playLen/speed,Math.max(0,(ov.trimEnd-ov.trimStart))));
      }
      if(ov.type==='audio' && exportFadeNode){
        const fadeNode=exportFadeNode;
        const audibleDuration=Math.max(.001,playLen/speed);
        let fadeIn=Math.max(0,Math.min(8,Number(audioCfg.fadeIn)||0));
        let fadeOut=Math.max(0,Math.min(8,Number(audioCfg.fadeOut)||0));
        if(fadeIn+fadeOut>audibleDuration){const factor=audibleDuration/(fadeIn+fadeOut);fadeIn*=factor;fadeOut*=factor;}
        try{
          fadeNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(when);
          fadeNode.gain.setValueAtTime(fadeIn>0?0:1,when);
          if(fadeIn>0)fadeNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1,when+fadeIn);
          if(fadeOut>0){const fadeStart=when+Math.max(fadeIn,audibleDuration-fadeOut);fadeNode.gain.setValueAtTime(1,fadeStart);fadeNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0,when+audibleDuration);}
        }catch(_e){fadeNode.gain.value=1;}
      }
      let nodeBuffer = audioBuf, nodeRate = speed, nodeOffset = sourceOffset, nodePlayLen = playLen;
      if(Math.abs(baseSpeed-1) > .001 && window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch){
        try{
          const stretched = await window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch.request(audioBuf, baseSpeed, sourceOffset, sourceOffset+playLen+.05);
          if(stretched && stretched.buffer){
            /* Time-stretched copy keeps the original pitch; only the semitone Pitch setting still changes the rate. */
            nodeBuffer = stretched.buffer;
            nodeRate = exportPitchRatio;
            nodeOffset = Math.max(0,(sourceOffset-stretched.originSec)/baseSpeed);
            nodePlayLen = Math.max(.001,playLen/baseSpeed);
          }
        }catch(stretchErr){console.warn('Ethvelle pitch-safe export fallback:',stretchErr);}
      }
      srcNode.buffer = nodeBuffer;
      srcNode.playbackRate.value = nodeRate;
      srcNode.start(when, nodeOffset, nodePlayLen);
      anyDecoded = true;
    }catch(err){
      const clipLabel=String(ov.name||ov.title||('Clip '+String(ov.id||'')));
      audioDecodeFailures.push(clipLabel);
      console.error('Ethvelle: could not decode audio for export; this clip will be silent',clipLabel,err);
    }
  }
  if(audioDecodeFailures.length){
    window.__ethvelleLastExportAudioDecodeFailures=audioDecodeFailures.slice();
    const shown=audioDecodeFailures.slice(0,3).join(', ');
    const more=audioDecodeFailures.length>3?' and '+(audioDecodeFailures.length-3)+' more':'';
    try{showToast('Audio could not be decoded for: '+shown+more,{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:4200});}catch(_e){}
  }else{window.__ethvelleLastExportAudioDecodeFailures=[];}
  if(!anyDecoded) return null;
  return await offlineCtx.startRendering();
}

// Load the pinned, browser-global muxer builds only when an export actually needs them.
function loadPinnedExportMuxer(globalName,src){
  if(window[globalName])return Promise.resolve(window[globalName]);
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
    const existing=document.querySelector('script[data-ethvelle-export-muxer="'+globalName+'"]');
    const script=existing||document.createElement('script');
    const done=()=>{if(window[globalName])resolve(window[globalName]);else reject(new Error('Export muxer did not initialize: '+globalName));};
    if(existing){
      if(existing.dataset.loaded==='1'){done();return;}
      existing.addEventListener('load',done,{once:true});
      existing.addEventListener('error',()=>reject(new Error('Could not load '+globalName)),{once:true});
      return;
    }
    script.src=src;script.async=true;script.dataset.ethvelleExportMuxer=globalName;
    script.onload=()=>{script.dataset.loaded='1';done();};
    script.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('Could not load pinned export muxer: '+globalName));
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });
}

// A video frame is never drawn into an export frame until the requested source time is
// decoded. Avoid seeking when the frame is already at the requested source time.
async function waitForExportVideoFrame(video,targetSec,clipLabel){
  if(!video)throw new Error('Missing video element for '+clipLabel);
  video.preload='auto';
  const target=Math.max(0,Number(targetSec)||0);
  const needsSeek=Math.abs((Number(video.currentTime)||0)-target)>0.008;
  if(needsSeek){
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      let settled=false;
      const timeout=setTimeout(()=>finish(new Error('Timed out decoding '+clipLabel)),12000);
      const cleanup=()=>{clearTimeout(timeout);['seeked','loadeddata','canplay','error'].forEach(name=>video.removeEventListener(name,onEvent));};
      const finish=err=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;cleanup();err?reject(err):resolve();};
      const onEvent=e=>{
        if(e.type==='error'){finish(new Error('Video decode failed for '+clipLabel));return;}
        if(video.readyState>=2&&(e.type==='seeked'||e.type==='loadeddata'||e.type==='canplay'))finish();
      };
      ['seeked','loadeddata','canplay','error'].forEach(name=>video.addEventListener(name,onEvent));
      try{video.currentTime=target;}catch(err){finish(err);}
    });
  }else if(video.readyState<2){
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      let settled=false;
      const timeout=setTimeout(()=>finish(new Error('Timed out loading '+clipLabel)),12000);
      const cleanup=()=>{clearTimeout(timeout);['loadeddata','canplay','error'].forEach(name=>video.removeEventListener(name,onEvent));};
      const finish=err=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;cleanup();err?reject(err):resolve();};
      const onEvent=e=>{if(e.type==='error')finish(new Error('Video decode failed for '+clipLabel));else if(video.readyState>=2)finish();};
      ['loadeddata','canplay','error'].forEach(name=>video.addEventListener(name,onEvent));
      if(video.readyState>=2)finish();
    });
  }
  if(video.readyState<2)throw new Error('No decoded video frame available for '+clipLabel);
}

async function startExport(){
  if(isExporting) return;
  // Hard safety checks against the signed-in user's actual plan limits — the export sheet
  // already prevents picking a locked quality or opening past the monthly cap, but this is
  // the last line of defense in case exportQuality was set before a plan change took effect.
  const plan = getMyPlanData();
  const maxQ = Number(plan.maxQuality) || 720;
  if(exportQuality > maxQ){
    showToast('Your plan only allows up to ' + maxQ + 'p exports — upgrade for higher quality', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:2600});
    return;
  }
  if(!plan.unlimitedExports && plan.maxExports != null){
    const used = getExportsUsedThisMonth();
    if(used >= plan.maxExports){
      showToast('You\u2019ve used all ' + plan.maxExports + ' exports included in your plan this month — upgrade for more', {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:3000});
      closeExportSheet();
      return;
    }
  }
  closeExportSheet();
  // v195: character-free and animation-free projects may still export backgrounds/media.
  const hasWebCodecsVideo=typeof VideoEncoder!=='undefined'&&typeof VideoFrame!=='undefined';
  if(!hasWebCodecsVideo&&typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined'){
    showToast("This browser can't export video. Try a recent version of Chrome.", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:2600});
    return;
  }
  const plannedDurationSec = getExportContentDurationSec();
  const renderFps = Math.max(24,Math.min(60,Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30));
  const requiredCredits = calculateExportCreditCost(exportQuality, plannedDurationSec);
  const wasPlaying = isPlaying;
  pausePlayback();
  isExporting = true;
  // v190: cover the editor with the exporting page right away. Credit verification and image
  // preloading below are async; without this the live canvas was visible for a moment first.
  try{
    const earlySize = qualityToHeight(exportQuality);
    window.__capExportCancelled = false;
    if(window.__capExportBegin) window.__capExportBegin(earlySize.w, earlySize.h);
    showExportProgress(0);
  }catch(_e){}
  let chargedCreditsAmount = 0;
  let creditChargeId = '';
  if(requiredCredits > 0){
    if(!(currentUser&&firebaseReady&&fbDb)){
      isExporting=false;
      try{hideExportProgress();}catch(_e){}
      showToast('Connect and sign in to verify your credit balance before exporting.', {icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2800});
      return;
    }
    try{
      const charge=await changeMyCreditBalance(-requiredCredits,{type:'usage',feature:'export',description:'Video export · '+(exportQuality===2160?'4K':exportQuality+'p')+' · '+Math.ceil(plannedDurationSec)+' sec',quality:exportQuality,durationSeconds:Math.ceil(plannedDurationSec)});
      chargedCreditsAmount=requiredCredits;creditChargeId=charge.id||'';
    }catch(err){
      isExporting=false;
      try{hideExportProgress();}catch(_e){}
      if(wasPlaying)startPlayback();
      if(err&&err.code==='ethvelle/insufficient-credits'){
        showToast('Not enough credits. Need '+requiredCredits+'; available '+myCreditsBalance+'. Upgrade your plan or ask admin to add credits.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3200});
      }else{
        showToast('Could not verify or deduct credits. Please try again after reconnecting.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3200});
      }
      renderMyPlanScreen();
      return;
    }
  }
  trackEvent('export_requested');
  // Exporting renders every frame of every clip in turn for every character, which
  // temporarily overwrites each character's own modelTransform (and bone angles, via
  // applyPoseToBones) via transform-keyframes/poses belonging to whichever frame is
  // currently being rendered — not necessarily where the live editor's playhead was. Save
  // every character's transform+pose here (not just the active one) so the whole cast can be
  // put back exactly as it looked before export started, even if a character's own clip has
  // no keyframe sitting at exactly the current playhead position to restore it via the
  // renderFrame() call in the `finally` block below.
  const preExportState = characters.map(c=>({
    modelTransform: { ...c.modelTransform },
    angles: Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(c.bones).map(k=>[k, c.bones[k].angle])),
    hipY: c.bones.hip.y
  }));
  let exportOverlayEls = {}; // ov.id -> { el, iw, ih } — declared here so `finally` can release the <video> elements even if export throws partway through
  let liveAudioCtx = null; // Used only by the MediaRecorder fallback when WebCodecs is unavailable.
  let exportVideoEncoder=null,exportAudioEncoder=null,exportMuxer=null,exportRecorder=null,exportCaptureTrack=null;
  let exportRecorderFinished=null,exportRecorderChunks=[],exportMode='webcodecs';

  try{
    const { w, h } = qualityToHeight(exportQuality);
    const exportCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    exportCanvas.width = w; exportCanvas.height = h;
    const exportCtx = exportCanvas.getContext('2d');
    // Every custom character's part images must be fully decoded BEFORE the frame loop
    // starts — drawCustomCharacterOnContext silently skips drawing any image that hasn't
    // finished loading yet (see its img.complete check), which would otherwise make a
    // custom character's limbs flicker in/missing for the first frame or two of export
    // while each Image() is still fetching its (already-in-memory, base64) src.
    await preloadCustomCharacterImages(exportCtx, characters);

    // The duration comes from the maximum real trimEnd across characters, backgrounds and
    // every overlay type; neither legacy 2s defaults nor padded timelineDuration are used.
    const durationSec = plannedDurationSec;
    const totalFrames = Math.max(1, Math.round(durationSec * renderFps));

    window.__capExportCancelled=false;window.__capExportBegin&&window.__capExportBegin(w,h);
    showExportProgress(0);

    // Build the full sample-accurate offline audio mix at the exact same content endpoint.
    let mixedAudioBuffer = null;
    try{ mixedAudioBuffer = await mixProjectAudioForExport(durationSec); }
    catch(err){
      console.error('Ethvelle: audio mix failed',err);
      showToast('Audio mix could not be prepared. The export will contain no audio.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:4200});
    }

    // FEATURE (multiple sequential backgrounds): pre-load EVERY background clip's image
    // once before the frame loop starts (mirrors exportOverlayEls just below) — not just
    // whichever one happened to be under the live playhead when export started. Solid-color
    // clips need no image at all; only url(...) clips get an Image() entry here.
    const exportBgEls = {}; // bg.id -> { el, iw, ih }
    for(const bg of backgrounds){
      if(!bg.src || !String(bg.src).startsWith('url(')) continue;
      const urlMatch = String(bg.src).match(/url\((.*)\)/);
      if(!urlMatch) continue;
      const img = new Image();
      img.src = urlMatch[1].replace(/^["']|["']$/g,'');
      await new Promise(res=>{ img.onload = res; img.onerror = res; });
      exportBgEls[bg.id] = { el: img, iw: img.naturalWidth||16, ih: img.naturalHeight||9 };
    }
    // Computes the same object-fit:cover destination rect + rotation the live preview's
    // applyBgTransform gives a background image, for whichever clip covers `tSec` — mirrors
    // drawOverlayMediaToContext's per-clip transform math just below, scoped to Background.
    function bgDrawFor(tSec){
      const bg = currentBackgroundClip(tSec);
      if(!bg) return { fill:'#ffffff', image:null, adjustments:null, enhanceAmount:0 };
      const rec = exportBgEls[bg.id];
      if(!rec) return { fill: bg.src && !String(bg.src).startsWith('url(') ? bg.src : '#ffffff', image:null, adjustments:bg.adjustments||null, enhanceAmount:bg.enhanceAmount||0 };
      const iw = rec.iw, ih = rec.ih;
      const bgExportScale = stageW ? (w/stageW) : 1;
      const t = ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(bg,'transform',tSec) || bg.transform || {scale:1, offsetX:0, offsetY:0, rotation:0};
      // UPDATED (no-crop backgrounds, export side): was Math.max(w/iw, h/ih) — "cover", which
      // scales the image up until it fills the frame and discards the overflow, i.e. crops it.
      // Math.min gives "contain": the largest size at which the WHOLE image still fits inside
      // the frame, identical to the live preview's `object-fit:contain` on .bg-img-layer, so the
      // exported video shows exactly the uncropped picture the editor shows.
      const crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(bg.crop):{left:0,top:0,right:0,bottom:0};
      const sourceX=iw*crop.left,sourceY=ih*crop.top;
      const sourceW=Math.max(1,iw*(1-crop.left-crop.right)),sourceH=Math.max(1,ih*(1-crop.top-crop.bottom));
      // Crop clips source pixels but keeps the same contain scale and source placement.
      // The cropped portion therefore never jumps/zooms to fill the whole project canvas.
      const baseScale=Math.min(w/iw,h/ih);
      const fullW=iw*baseScale,fullH=ih*baseScale;
      const baseW=sourceW*baseScale,baseH=sourceH*baseScale;
      const destX=(w-fullW)/2+sourceX*baseScale;
      const destY=(h-fullH)/2+sourceY*baseScale;
      return {
        fill: null,
        image: rec.el,
        sourceX,sourceY,sourceW,sourceH,
        destX,destY,baseW,baseH,
        offsetX:(Number(t.offsetX)||0)*bgExportScale,
        offsetY:(Number(t.offsetY)||0)*bgExportScale,
        scale:Number(t.scale)||1,
        rotation: Number(t.rotation)||0, mirrorX:!!t.mirrorX, mirrorY:!!t.mirrorY, visualMask:bg.visualMask||null, adjustments:bg.adjustments||null, enhanceAmount:bg.enhanceAmount||0,
        perspectiveX:Number(bg.mediaPerspectiveX)||0, perspectiveY:Number(bg.mediaPerspectiveY)||0, perspectiveScale:bgExportScale
      };
    }

    // BUGFIX (added clips didn't show on the background — export side): mirrors
    // renderOverlayMedia()'s live-preview compositing. Every video/image overlay clip gets
    // its own offscreen <video>/<img> element here, pre-loaded once before the frame loop
    // starts (not created fresh per-frame — that would mean re-fetching/re-decoding the same
    // source dozens of times a second). Each is object-fit:cover-sized to the export canvas
    // exactly like .overlay-media-layer's CSS does in the live DOM, then drawn in `overlays`
    // array order (later clips paint over earlier ones) between the background and the
    // stickman on every frame where that clip's [trimStart, trimEnd) covers the current time.
    exportOverlayEls = {}; // ov.id -> { el, iw, ih } for video/image overlays
    const exportChromaCanvases = {}; // reusable processed frames for chroma-keyed videos
    try{ if(document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) await document.fonts.ready; }catch(e){}
    for(const ov of overlays){
      if(ov.type!=='video' && ov.type!=='image') continue;
      if(ov.type==='video'){
        const v = document.createElement('video');
        v.preload='auto';v.crossOrigin='anonymous';v.muted = true; v.playsInline = true;v.loop=false;
        v.src = ov.src;
        await new Promise(res=>{ v.onloadedmetadata = res; v.onerror = res; });
        exportOverlayEls[ov.id] = { el:v, iw:v.videoWidth||16, ih:v.videoHeight||9 };
      } else {
        const img = new Image();
        img.crossOrigin='anonymous';img.src = ov.src;
        await new Promise(res=>{ img.onload = res; img.onerror = res; });
        exportOverlayEls[ov.id] = { el:img, iw:img.naturalWidth||16, ih:img.naturalHeight||9 };
      }
    }
    const baseBitrate = exportQuality >= 2160 ? 16000000 : exportQuality >= 1440 ? 12000000 : exportQuality >= 1080 ? 8000000 : exportQuality >= 720 ? 5000000 : 2500000;
    const bitrateMultiplier = window.exportBitrateMode === 'high' ? 1.4 : 1;
    const customBitrate = Number(window.exportBitrateMbps);
    const outputBitrate = customBitrate > 0 ? Math.round(customBitrate * 1000000) : Math.round(baseBitrate * bitrateMultiplier * (renderFps/30));
    const wantsAudio=!!mixedAudioBuffer;
    const canUseWebCodecs=typeof VideoEncoder!=='undefined'&&typeof VideoFrame!=='undefined'&&(!wantsAudio||(typeof AudioEncoder!=='undefined'&&typeof AudioData!=='undefined'));
    let exportOutputMime='video/webm',exportFileExtension='webm',exportVideoConfig=null,exportAudioConfig=null;
    let muxerTarget=null;
    if(canUseWebCodecs){
      const probeVideo=async configs=>{
        for(const cfg of configs){try{const result=await VideoEncoder.isConfigSupported(cfg);if(result&&result.supported)return result.config||cfg;}catch(_e){}}
        return null;
      };
      const probeAudio=async cfg=>{if(!wantsAudio)return null;try{const result=await AudioEncoder.isConfigSupported(cfg);return result&&result.supported?(result.config||cfg):null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
      const mp4Video=await probeVideo([
        {codec:'avc1.420033',width:w,height:h,bitrate:outputBitrate,framerate:renderFps,latencyMode:'realtime'},
        {codec:'avc1.420028',width:w,height:h,bitrate:outputBitrate,framerate:renderFps,latencyMode:'realtime'},
        {codec:'avc1.42001f',width:w,height:h,bitrate:outputBitrate,framerate:renderFps,latencyMode:'realtime'}
      ]);
      const aacConfig=await probeAudio({codec:'mp4a.40.2',sampleRate:mixedAudioBuffer?mixedAudioBuffer.sampleRate:44100,numberOfChannels:mixedAudioBuffer?mixedAudioBuffer.numberOfChannels:2,bitrate:160000});
      if(mp4Video&&(!wantsAudio||aacConfig)){
        const lib=await loadPinnedExportMuxer('Mp4Muxer','https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/mp4-muxer@5.2.2/build/mp4-muxer.js');
        if(!lib||!lib.Muxer||!lib.ArrayBufferTarget)throw new Error('The MP4 muxer library is unavailable.');
        muxerTarget=new lib.ArrayBufferTarget();
        exportMuxer=new lib.Muxer({target:muxerTarget,video:{codec:'avc',width:w,height:h,frameRate:renderFps},...(wantsAudio?{audio:{codec:'aac',numberOfChannels:mixedAudioBuffer.numberOfChannels,sampleRate:mixedAudioBuffer.sampleRate}}:{}),fastStart:'in-memory'});
        exportOutputMime='video/mp4';exportFileExtension='mp4';exportVideoConfig=mp4Video;exportAudioConfig=aacConfig;
      }else{
        const webmVideo=await probeVideo([
          {codec:'vp09.00.10.08',width:w,height:h,bitrate:outputBitrate,framerate:renderFps,latencyMode:'realtime'},
          {codec:'vp8',width:w,height:h,bitrate:outputBitrate,framerate:renderFps,latencyMode:'realtime'}
        ]);
        const webmAudio=await probeAudio({codec:'opus',sampleRate:mixedAudioBuffer?mixedAudioBuffer.sampleRate:44100,numberOfChannels:mixedAudioBuffer?mixedAudioBuffer.numberOfChannels:2,bitrate:128000});
        if(!webmVideo)throw new Error('This browser does not support a compatible WebCodecs video encoder for this export size.');
        if(wantsAudio&&!webmAudio)throw new Error('This browser cannot encode the project audio as Opus or AAC.');
        const lib=await loadPinnedExportMuxer('WebMMuxer','https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/webm-muxer@5.1.4/build/webm-muxer.js');
        if(!lib||!lib.Muxer||!lib.ArrayBufferTarget)throw new Error('The WebM muxer library is unavailable.');
        muxerTarget=new lib.ArrayBufferTarget();
        const codec=String(webmVideo.codec).startsWith('vp09')?'V_VP9':'V_VP8';
        exportMuxer=new lib.Muxer({target:muxerTarget,video:{codec,width:w,height:h,frameRate:renderFps},...(wantsAudio?{audio:{codec:'A_OPUS',numberOfChannels:mixedAudioBuffer.numberOfChannels,sampleRate:mixedAudioBuffer.sampleRate}}:{})});
        exportOutputMime='video/webm';exportFileExtension='webm';exportVideoConfig=webmVideo;exportAudioConfig=webmAudio;
      }
      let videoEncoderError=null,audioEncoderError=null;
      exportVideoEncoder=new VideoEncoder({output:(chunk,meta)=>exportMuxer.addVideoChunk(chunk,meta),error:err=>{videoEncoderError=err;}});
      exportVideoEncoder.configure(exportVideoConfig);
      if(wantsAudio){
        exportAudioEncoder=new AudioEncoder({output:(chunk,meta)=>exportMuxer.addAudioChunk(chunk,meta),error:err=>{audioEncoderError=err;}});
        exportAudioEncoder.configure(exportAudioConfig);
      }
      window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors=()=>({video:videoEncoderError,audio:audioEncoderError});
      exportMode='webcodecs';
    }else{
      // Compatibility fallback only when WebCodecs (or the required audio encoder pieces)
      // are absent. requestFrame() is called only after the complete scratch frame is drawn.
      if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined')throw new Error('WebCodecs is unavailable and MediaRecorder is not supported.');
      exportMode='mediarecorder';
      const stream=exportCanvas.captureStream(0);
      exportCaptureTrack=stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
      if(!exportCaptureTrack||typeof exportCaptureTrack.requestFrame!=='function')throw new Error('This browser does not support manual canvas frame capture.');
      if(wantsAudio){
        liveAudioCtx=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const destination=liveAudioCtx.createMediaStreamDestination();
        destination.stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(track=>stream.addTrack(track));
        liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode=liveAudioCtx.createBufferSource();
        liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode.buffer=mixedAudioBuffer;
        liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode.connect(destination);
        try{await liveAudioCtx.resume();}catch(_e){}
      }
      const mimeCandidates=['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9','video/webm;codecs=vp8','video/webm'];
      const mimeType=mimeCandidates.find(m=>MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported&&MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(m))||'video/webm';
      exportOutputMime=mimeType;exportFileExtension=mimeType.includes('mp4')?'mp4':'webm';
      exportRecorder=new MediaRecorder(stream,{mimeType,videoBitsPerSecond:outputBitrate});
      exportRecorder.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size>0)exportRecorderChunks.push(e.data);};
      exportRecorderFinished=new Promise(resolve=>{exportRecorder.onstop=resolve;});
    }

    // Draws every overlay clip active at `tSec` onto the offscreen scratch canvas, preserving
    // the original export order. The scratch canvas is encoded only after ALL layers finish.
    async function drawOverlayMediaToContext(tSec, behindBackgroundPass){
      const isBehindPass=behindBackgroundPass===true;
      const active = overlays.filter(o=> (o.type==='video'||o.type==='image'||o.type==='text') && tSec >= o.trimStart && tSec < o.trimEnd && !!o.__ethvelleBehindBackground===isBehindPass);
      for(const ov of active){
        if(ov.type==='text'){
          const exportScale=stageW ? (w/stageW) : 1;
          const tr=ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',tSec)||ov.transform||{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
          const fontSize=Math.max(8,Number(ov.fontSize)||36)*exportScale;
          const evAnim = window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState ? window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,tSec) : {opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
          exportCtx.save();
          const overallTextOpacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.textOverallOpacity==null?100:ov.textOverallOpacity)))/100;
          exportCtx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(evAnim.opacity)||0))*overallTextOpacity;
          exportCtx.filter = window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter ? window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(ov.adjustments,ov.enhanceAmount||0) : 'none';
          exportCtx.translate(w/2+((Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(evAnim.x)||0))*exportScale,h/2+((Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(evAnim.y)||0))*exportScale);
          exportCtx.rotate(((Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(evAnim.rotation)||0))*Math.PI/180);
          const exportObjectScale = (Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(evAnim.scale)||1);
          exportCtx.scale(exportObjectScale,exportObjectScale);
          exportCtx.scale(ov.mirrorX?-1:1,ov.mirrorY?-1:1);
          if(ov.visualMask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip){exportCtx.save();exportCtx.translate(-w/2,-h/2);window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(exportCtx,ov.visualMask,w,h);exportCtx.translate(w/2,h/2);}
          exportCtx.fillStyle=ethvelleHexToRgba(ov.textColor||'#ffffff',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.textOpacity==null?100:ov.textOpacity)))/100);
          exportCtx.textAlign=ov.textAlign||'center'; exportCtx.textBaseline='middle';
          exportCtx.font=`${ov.fontStyle==='italic'?'italic ':''}${Number(ov.fontWeight)||800} ${fontSize}px "${String(ov.fontFamily||'Inter').replace(/["\\]/g,'')}", sans-serif`;
          const maxWidth=w*0.82, lineHeight=fontSize*1.2, lines=[];
          const strokeWidth=Math.max(0,Number(ov.strokeWidth)||0)*exportScale;
          const strokeColor=ethvelleHexToRgba(ov.strokeColor||'#17172B',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.strokeOpacity==null?100:ov.strokeOpacity)))/100);
          const shadowEnabled=!!ov.shadowEnabled;
          const shadowBlur=Math.max(0,Number(ov.shadowBlur)||0)*exportScale;
          const shadowX=(Number(ov.shadowX)||0)*exportScale;
          const shadowY=(Math.max(4,Number(ov.shadowY)||6))*exportScale;
          const bgPad=Math.max(0,Number(ov.textBackgroundPadding==null?7:ov.textBackgroundPadding))*exportScale;
          const bgRadius=Math.max(0,Number(ov.textBackgroundRadius==null?8:ov.textBackgroundRadius))*exportScale;
          const bgFill=ov.textBackgroundEnabled?ethvelleHexToRgba(ov.textBackgroundColor||'#182A66',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.textBackgroundOpacity==null?78:ov.textBackgroundOpacity)))/100):'transparent';
          const drawRoundedRect=(x,y,rw,rh,r)=>{
            const rr=Math.max(0,Math.min(r,rw/2,rh/2));
            exportCtx.beginPath(); exportCtx.moveTo(x+rr,y); exportCtx.lineTo(x+rw-rr,y); exportCtx.quadraticCurveTo(x+rw,y,x+rw,y+rr); exportCtx.lineTo(x+rw,y+rh-rr); exportCtx.quadraticCurveTo(x+rw,y+rh,x+rw-rr,y+rh); exportCtx.lineTo(x+rr,y+rh); exportCtx.quadraticCurveTo(x,y+rh,x,y+rh-rr); exportCtx.lineTo(x,y+rr); exportCtx.quadraticCurveTo(x,y,x+rr,y); exportCtx.closePath(); exportCtx.fill();
          };
          String(ov.textContent||'').split('\n').forEach(paragraph=>{
            const words=paragraph.split(/\s+/); let line='';
            words.forEach(word=>{
              const candidate=line ? line+' '+word : word;
              if(line && exportCtx.measureText(candidate).width>maxWidth){lines.push(line);line=word;}else line=candidate;
            });
            lines.push(line);
          });
          const usable=lines.filter((line,i)=>line!=='' || lines.length===1);
          const totalH=usable.length*lineHeight;
          const bgShape=String(ov.textBackgroundShape||'rounded');
          if(ov.textBackgroundEnabled&&bgShape!=='rounded'&&typeof window.ethvelleDrawTextBackplate==='function'){
            const widest=Math.max(1,...usable.map(line=>Math.min(maxWidth,exportCtx.measureText(line).width)));
            const bx=(ov.textAlign||'center')==='left'?-maxWidth/2-bgPad:(ov.textAlign==='right'?maxWidth/2-widest-bgPad:-widest/2-bgPad);
            exportCtx.save();exportCtx.shadowColor='transparent';exportCtx.shadowBlur=0;exportCtx.shadowOffsetX=0;exportCtx.shadowOffsetY=0;exportCtx.fillStyle=bgFill;
            window.ethvelleDrawTextBackplate(exportCtx,bx,-totalH/2-bgPad,widest+bgPad*2,totalH+bgPad*2,bgRadius,bgShape);
            exportCtx.restore();
          }
          usable.forEach((line,i)=>{
            const y=-totalH/2+lineHeight*(i+0.5);
            const lineW=Math.min(maxWidth,exportCtx.measureText(line).width);
            let lineX=0;
            if((ov.textAlign||'center')==='left') lineX=-maxWidth/2;
            else if((ov.textAlign||'center')==='right') lineX=maxWidth/2;
            if(ov.textBackgroundEnabled&&bgShape==='rounded'){
              exportCtx.save(); exportCtx.shadowColor='transparent'; exportCtx.shadowBlur=0; exportCtx.shadowOffsetX=0; exportCtx.shadowOffsetY=0;
              exportCtx.fillStyle=bgFill;
              const rectX=(ov.textAlign||'center')==='left' ? -maxWidth/2-bgPad : (ov.textAlign==='right' ? maxWidth/2-lineW-bgPad : -lineW/2-bgPad);
              drawRoundedRect(rectX,y-lineHeight/2-bgPad,lineW+bgPad*2,lineHeight+bgPad*2,bgRadius);
              exportCtx.restore();
            }
            exportCtx.shadowColor=shadowEnabled?ethvelleHexToRgba(ov.shadowColor||'#000000',Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(ov.shadowOpacity==null?100:ov.shadowOpacity)))/100):'transparent';
            exportCtx.shadowBlur=shadowEnabled?shadowBlur:0;
            exportCtx.shadowOffsetX=shadowEnabled?shadowX:0;
            exportCtx.shadowOffsetY=shadowEnabled?shadowY:0;
            if(strokeWidth>0){ exportCtx.lineWidth=strokeWidth*2; exportCtx.lineJoin='round'; exportCtx.strokeStyle=strokeColor; if(window.ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth)window.ethvelleDrawStrokeDepth(exportCtx,line,lineX,y,maxWidth,strokeWidth,exportScale); exportCtx.strokeText(line,lineX,y,maxWidth); }
            exportCtx.fillText(line,lineX,y,maxWidth);
          });
          if(ov.visualMask&&window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip)exportCtx.restore();
          exportCtx.restore();
          continue;
        }
        const rec = exportOverlayEls[ov.id];
        if(!rec) continue;
        if(ov.type==='video'){
          const speed = ov.speed || 1;
          // Speed-scaled the same way as the live preview (see renderOverlayMedia) so an
          // exported video matches exactly what was seen in the editor.
          const maxT = Math.max(0.05, (ov.sourceDuration || rec.el.duration || (ov.trimEnd-ov.trimStart)) - 0.02);
          const elapsed=Math.max(0,(tSec-Number(ov.trimStart))*speed);
          const span=Math.max(0,(Number(ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov.trimStart))*speed);
          const rawLocal=ov.reversePlayback?(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+span-elapsed:(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+elapsed;
          const localT = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxT, rawLocal));
          await waitForExportVideoFrame(rec.el,localT,ov.name||('Video clip '+String(ov.id||'')));
        }
        const evAnim = window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState ? window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,tSec) : {opacity:1,scale:1,x:0,y:0,rotation:0};
        const coverScale = Math.min(w/rec.iw, h/rec.ih) * (ov.transform ? ov.transform.scale : 1) * (Number(evAnim.scale)||1);
        const drawW = rec.iw*coverScale, drawH = rec.ih*coverScale;
        // Mirrors the live preview's authored transform plus the selected clip's animation.
        const ovExportScale = stageW ? (w/stageW) : 1;
        const trackedExportTransform = window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform ? window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,tSec) : ov.transform;
        const keyedExportTransform = ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(ov)==='transform' ? ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',tSec) : null;
        const t = ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId ? trackedExportTransform : (keyedExportTransform||trackedExportTransform);
        const cx = w/2 + (((t ? Number(t.offsetX)||0 : 0) + (Number(evAnim.x)||0))*ovExportScale);
        const cy = h/2 + (((t ? Number(t.offsetY)||0 : 0) + (Number(evAnim.y)||0))*ovExportScale);
        const exportRotation = (t ? Number(t.rotation)||0 : 0) + (Number(evAnim.rotation)||0);
        try{
          exportCtx.save();
          exportCtx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(evAnim.opacity)||0));
          const exportBaseFilter=window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter ? window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(ov.adjustments, ov.enhanceAmount) : 'none';
          const exportBlurFx=window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect?window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect(ov,tSec):(ov.visualEffects&&ov.visualEffects.type==='blur'?ov.visualEffects:null);
          const exportBlurPx=exportBlurFx ? Math.max(0,Math.min(14,(Number(exportBlurFx.intensity)||135)/30))*ovExportScale : 0;
          exportCtx.filter=exportBlurPx>0 ? ((exportBaseFilter&&exportBaseFilter!=='none'?exportBaseFilter+' ':'')+'blur('+exportBlurPx.toFixed(2)+'px)') : exportBaseFilter;
          let drawSource=rec.el;
          if(ov.type==='video' && ov.chromaKey && ov.chromaKey.enabled){
            let keyed=exportChromaCanvases[ov.id];
            if(!keyed){ keyed=document.createElement('canvas'); exportChromaCanvases[ov.id]=keyed; }
            if(paintChromaKeyFrame(rec.el,keyed,ov.chromaKey,1280)) drawSource=keyed;
          }
          const crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(ov.crop):{left:0,top:0,right:0,bottom:0};
          const sourceX=rec.iw*crop.left,sourceY=rec.ih*crop.top;
          const sourceW=Math.max(1,rec.iw*(1-crop.left-crop.right)),sourceH=Math.max(1,rec.ih*(1-crop.top-crop.bottom));
          // Preserve the full source's contain scale. The crop only removes pixels from the
          // original source rectangle; it does not enlarge or recenter the remaining pixels.
          const baseContainScale=Math.min(w/rec.iw,h/rec.ih);
          const fullDrawW=rec.iw*baseContainScale,fullDrawH=rec.ih*baseContainScale;
          const baseDrawW=sourceW*baseContainScale,baseDrawH=sourceH*baseContainScale;
          const cropDestX=(w-fullDrawW)/2+sourceX*baseContainScale;
          const cropDestY=(h-fullDrawH)/2+sourceY*baseContainScale;
          const objectScale=(t?(Number(t.scale)||1):1)*(Number(evAnim.scale)||1);
          exportCtx.save();
          exportCtx.translate(cx,cy);
          if(exportRotation)exportCtx.rotate(exportRotation*Math.PI/180);
          exportCtx.scale(objectScale*(ov.mirrorX?-1:1),objectScale*(ov.mirrorY?-1:1));
          exportCtx.translate(-w/2,-h/2);
          // The mask is clipped in the transformed layer's own full-stage box, so it moves,
          // rotates and mirrors together with the image/video exactly like the live CSS mask.
          if(window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip)window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(exportCtx,ov.visualMask,w,h);
          const evPerspX=Number(ov.mediaPerspectiveX)||0,evPerspY=Number(ov.mediaPerspectiveY)||0;
          if((evPerspX||evPerspY)&&window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage)window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage(exportCtx,drawSource,sourceX,sourceY,sourceW,sourceH,cropDestX,cropDestY,baseDrawW,baseDrawH,evPerspX,evPerspY,w/2,h/2,(window.ethvelleMediaPerspectiveDistance||900)*ovExportScale);
          else exportCtx.drawImage(drawSource,sourceX,sourceY,sourceW,sourceH,cropDestX,cropDestY,baseDrawW,baseDrawH);
          exportCtx.restore();
          exportCtx.restore();
        }
        catch(err){ try{exportCtx.restore();}catch(_e){} throw new Error('Could not draw '+String(ov.name||('clip '+ov.id))+': '+(err&&err.message||err)); }
      }
    }

    // Render each frame of the FULL combined timeline (every clip/segment in its own
    // absolute-time slot) into the export canvas at the target FPS — using the exact same
    // poseAtClipTime()/transformAtClipTime() functions the live preview uses, so exported
    // motion always matches what was seen in the editor (PART 35). animateAllCharacters()
    // additionally poses every OTHER character from its own clip each frame, so multiple
    // characters with their own animations all move together in the exported video instead
    // of only the active one animating while the rest export frozen in their rest pose.
    if(exportMode==='mediarecorder'){
      exportRecorder.start();
      if(liveAudioCtx&&liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode){try{liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode.start();}catch(err){throw new Error('Could not start fallback export audio: '+err.message);}}
    }else if(exportMode==='webcodecs'&&mixedAudioBuffer&&exportAudioEncoder){
      // Encode offline-mixed PCM with sample-derived timestamps, not wall-clock playback.
      const channels=mixedAudioBuffer.numberOfChannels,sampleRate=mixedAudioBuffer.sampleRate,totalSamples=mixedAudioBuffer.length;
      const channelData=Array.from({length:channels},(_,i)=>mixedAudioBuffer.getChannelData(i));
      const chunkFrames=1024;
      for(let offset=0;offset<totalSamples;offset+=chunkFrames){
        const frameCount=Math.min(chunkFrames,totalSamples-offset);
        const planar=new Float32Array(frameCount*channels);
        for(let ch=0;ch<channels;ch++)planar.set(channelData[ch].subarray(offset,offset+frameCount),ch*frameCount);
        const audioData=new AudioData({format:'f32-planar',sampleRate,numberOfFrames:frameCount,numberOfChannels:channels,timestamp:Math.round(offset*1000000/sampleRate),data:planar});
        try{exportAudioEncoder.encode(audioData);}finally{audioData.close();}
        if(exportAudioEncoder.encodeQueueSize>24)await exportAudioEncoder.flush();
        const audioError=window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors&&window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors().audio;
        if(audioError)throw audioError;
      }
      await exportAudioEncoder.flush();
      const audioError=window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors&&window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors().audio;
      if(audioError)throw audioError;
    }

    const fallbackStartMs=exportMode==='mediarecorder'?performance.now():0;
    const frameIntervalMs=1000/renderFps;
    let fallbackFrameIndex=0;
    for(let f=0;exportMode==='webcodecs'?f<totalFrames:true;f++){
      let clipAbsoluteT;
      if(exportMode==='webcodecs')clipAbsoluteT=f/renderFps;
      else{
        const elapsed=Math.max(0,(performance.now()-fallbackStartMs)/1000);
        clipAbsoluteT=elapsed>=durationSec?Math.max(0,durationSec-1/renderFps):Math.min(elapsed,Math.max(0,durationSec-1/renderFps));
      }
      faceAnimationTimeOverride=clipAbsoluteT;
      if(activeCharacter()) resetCharacterForTimelineFrame(activeCharacter());
      if(activeCharacter() && activeClip){
        const pose = poseAtClipTime(clipAbsoluteT);
        if(pose) applyPoseToBones(pose);
        const clipTransform = transformAtClipTime(clipAbsoluteT);
        if(clipTransform) applyTransformToModel(clipTransform);
      }
      if(window.__capExportCancelled)throw new Error('export-cancelled');
      animateAllCharacters(clipAbsoluteT);

      exportCtx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
      // Paint overlays explicitly sent behind the Background first. The background is then
      // drawn over them, matching the live canvas z-order instead of letting export silently
      // pull every overlay back above the background layer.
      await drawOverlayMediaToContext(clipAbsoluteT,true);
      // FEATURE (multiple sequential backgrounds): resolved freshly for THIS frame's own
      // time — a project that switches background partway through now actually shows that
      // switch in the exported video, instead of exporting whichever background was active
      // only at the moment export started.
      const bgNow = bgDrawFor(clipAbsoluteT);
      if(bgNow.image){
        exportCtx.save();
        // Keep the stage's neutral white visible outside a masked/moved background image.
        exportCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; exportCtx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
        exportCtx.filter = window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter ? window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(bgNow.adjustments, bgNow.enhanceAmount) : 'none';
        exportCtx.translate(w/2+bgNow.offsetX,h/2+bgNow.offsetY);
        if(bgNow.rotation) exportCtx.rotate(bgNow.rotation * Math.PI/180);
        exportCtx.scale(bgNow.scale*(bgNow.mirrorX?-1:1),bgNow.scale*(bgNow.mirrorY?-1:1));
        exportCtx.translate(-w/2,-h/2);
        if(window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip) window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(exportCtx,bgNow.visualMask,w,h);
        if((bgNow.perspectiveX||bgNow.perspectiveY)&&window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage)window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage(exportCtx,bgNow.image,bgNow.sourceX||0,bgNow.sourceY||0,bgNow.sourceW||bgNow.image.width,bgNow.sourceH||bgNow.image.height,bgNow.destX??((w-bgNow.baseW)/2),bgNow.destY??((h-bgNow.baseH)/2),bgNow.baseW,bgNow.baseH,bgNow.perspectiveX,bgNow.perspectiveY,w/2,h/2,(window.ethvelleMediaPerspectiveDistance||900)*(bgNow.perspectiveScale||1));
        else exportCtx.drawImage(bgNow.image,bgNow.sourceX||0,bgNow.sourceY||0,bgNow.sourceW||bgNow.image.width,bgNow.sourceH||bgNow.image.height,bgNow.destX??((w-bgNow.baseW)/2),bgNow.destY??((h-bgNow.baseH)/2),bgNow.baseW,bgNow.baseH);
        exportCtx.restore();
      }
      else {
        exportCtx.save();
        exportCtx.filter = window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter ? window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(bgNow.adjustments, bgNow.enhanceAmount) : 'none';
        exportCtx.fillStyle = bgNow.fill; exportCtx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
        exportCtx.restore();
      }

      await drawOverlayMediaToContext(clipAbsoluteT,false);

      drawStickToContext(exportCtx, w, h);
      if(window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext) window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext(exportCtx,clipAbsoluteT,w,h);

      if(exportMode==='webcodecs'){
        const timestamp=Math.round(f*1000000/renderFps);
        const frame=new VideoFrame(exportCanvas,{timestamp,duration:Math.round(1000000/renderFps)});
        try{exportVideoEncoder.encode(frame,{keyFrame:f===0||f%(renderFps*2)===0});}finally{frame.close();}
        while(exportVideoEncoder.encodeQueueSize>8){await new Promise(r=>{let d=false;const fin=()=>{if(d)return;d=true;exportVideoEncoder.ondequeue=null;r();};exportVideoEncoder.ondequeue=fin;setTimeout(fin,40);});if(window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors&&(window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors().video))break;}
        const encoderErrors=window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors&&window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors();
        if(encoderErrors&&encoderErrors.video)throw encoderErrors.video;
        if(f%Math.max(1,Math.floor(renderFps/2))===0){showExportProgress(Math.round(((f+1)/totalFrames)*96));window.__capExportFrame&&window.__capExportFrame(exportCanvas);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));}
      }else{
        // MediaRecorder timestamps from the real clock. The source time is also taken from
        // that clock, and the offline mix starts with recorder.start(), keeping this fallback
        // in sync even when the renderer cannot maintain the requested frame rate.
        exportCaptureTrack.requestFrame();
        fallbackFrameIndex++;
        const elapsed=(performance.now()-fallbackStartMs)/1000;
        showExportProgress(Math.min(96,Math.round(elapsed/durationSec*96)));window.__capExportFrame&&window.__capExportFrame(exportCanvas);
        if(elapsed>=durationSec)break;
        const nextDue=fallbackStartMs+fallbackFrameIndex*frameIntervalMs;
        const waitMs=nextDue-performance.now();
        if(waitMs>1)await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      }
    }

    let blob;
    if(exportMode==='webcodecs'){
      await exportVideoEncoder.flush();
      const encoderErrors=window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors&&window.__ethvelleExportEncoderErrors();
      if(encoderErrors&&(encoderErrors.video||encoderErrors.audio))throw (encoderErrors.video||encoderErrors.audio);
      exportMuxer.finalize();
      const finalBuffer=muxerTarget&&muxerTarget.buffer;
      if(!finalBuffer||!finalBuffer.byteLength)throw new Error('The video encoder produced an empty export.');
      blob=new Blob([finalBuffer],{type:exportOutputMime});
    }else{
      exportRecorder.stop();
      await exportRecorderFinished;
      blob=new Blob(exportRecorderChunks,{type:exportOutputMime||'video/webm'});
    }
    showExportProgress(100);
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const safeName = (currentProject?.name || 'ethvelle-animation').replace(/[^a-z0-9\-_]+/gi,'_');
    const savedFileName = `${safeName}-${exportQuality}p.${exportFileExtension}`;
    // Direct save: the file is written to the device (Downloads) automatically the moment export ends.
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = savedFileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    if(window.__capExportDone) window.__capExportDone({blob, url, filename:savedFileName}); else { hideExportProgress(); showToast('Saved to device', {icon: toastIcons.export, duration:1800}); }
    recordExportUsed();
    trackEvent('export');
  }catch(err){
    console.error('Ethvelle export failed', err);
    hideExportProgress();
    trackEvent('export_failed');
    if(chargedCreditsAmount>0){
      try{await changeMyCreditBalance(chargedCreditsAmount,{type:'refund',feature:'export',description:'Refund for failed video export',quality:exportQuality,durationSeconds:Math.ceil(plannedDurationSec),referenceId:creditChargeId});}
      catch(refundErr){console.error('Ethvelle: automatic credit refund failed',refundErr);}
    }
    showToast((err&&err.message==='export-cancelled')?"Export cancelled — credits refunded when possible":"Export failed — credits were refunded when possible. Try a lower quality", {icon: toastIcons.info, duration:3000});
  }finally{
    faceAnimationTimeOverride=null;
    isExporting = false;
    // Release the offscreen <video> elements created for overlay export compositing —
    // without this, each export would leak a decoder/video element per video overlay clip.
    Object.values(exportOverlayEls).forEach(rec=>{
      if(rec.el.tagName === 'VIDEO'){ rec.el.pause(); rec.el.removeAttribute('src'); rec.el.load(); }
    });
    // Tear down the real-time audio-mix context (if the project had any sound) — stopping
    // the source node is harmless even if it already finished playing on its own (a
    // buffer source that's already ended throws on a second stop() in some browsers, so
    // this is wrapped defensively), and closing the context releases its audio-processing
    // resources instead of leaving one AudioContext alive per export.
    if(exportRecorder&&exportRecorder.state!=='inactive'){try{exportRecorder.stop();}catch(_e){}}
    if(exportVideoEncoder){try{if(exportVideoEncoder.state!=='closed')exportVideoEncoder.close();}catch(_e){}}
    if(exportAudioEncoder){try{if(exportAudioEncoder.state!=='closed')exportAudioEncoder.close();}catch(_e){}}
    if(liveAudioCtx){
      try{ if(liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode) liveAudioCtx._exportSourceNode.stop(); }catch(e){}
      try{ liveAudioCtx.close(); }catch(e){}
    }
    // Put every character's modelTransform AND bone pose back exactly as they were before
    // export touched them, then let renderFrame() re-apply whatever pose/transform-keyframe
    // (if any) actually belongs at the current playhead on top of that — for the active
    // character via the shared bones/modelTransform, and for every other character via
    // animateAllCharacters() inside that same renderFrame() call.
    characters.forEach((c, idx)=>{
      const saved = preExportState[idx];
      if(!saved) return;
      Object.assign(c.modelTransform, saved.modelTransform);
      Object.keys(saved.angles).forEach(k=>{ if(c.bones[k]) c.bones[k].angle = saved.angles[k]; });
      c.bones.hip.y = saved.hipY;
    });
    // restore live canvas to current playhead position
    renderFrame();
    if(wasPlaying) startPlayback();
  }
}

// Draw the current bone pose into an arbitrary canvas context, scaled to that context's size.
// Draws ONE character's rig (given the character object itself, independent of whichever
// character is currently "active") into an arbitrary target context, scaled to that
// context's size. Shared by drawStickToContext (loops this over every character) so export/
// thumbnails render the whole cast, not just whichever one happens to be selected. Takes the
// full character (not just bones/modelTransform) so a custom image-based character exports
// with its own artwork (see drawCustomCharacterOnContext) instead of always falling back to
// the green stroke stickman.
function drawCharacterToContext(targetCtx, char){
  const charBones = char.bones, charModelTransform = char.modelTransform;
  function boneEnd(key){
    const b = charBones[key];
    if(!b.parent) return {x:b.x, y:b.y};
    const pEnd = boneEnd(b.parent);
    let sx=pEnd.x, sy=pEnd.y;
    if(b.attachAt!==undefined){
      const pStart = boneStart(b.parent);
      sx = pStart.x + (pEnd.x-pStart.x)*b.attachAt;
      sy = pStart.y + (pEnd.y-pStart.y)*b.attachAt;
    }
    return { x: sx+Math.cos(b.angle)*b.length, y: sy+Math.sin(b.angle)*b.length };
  }
  function boneStart(key){
    const b = charBones[key];
    if(!b.parent) return {x:b.x, y:b.y};
    const pEnd = boneEnd(b.parent);
    if(b.attachAt!==undefined){
      const pStart = boneStart(b.parent);
      return { x: pStart.x+(pEnd.x-pStart.x)*b.attachAt, y: pStart.y+(pEnd.y-pStart.y)*b.attachAt };
    }
    return pEnd;
  }
  targetCtx.save();
  const anchor = charBones.hip;
