function sheetLabel(k){
  const type = ED.modelType || 'humanoid';
  if(type === 'animal'){
    const labels={head:'Head / muzzle',torso:'Body',tail:'Tail',armL_upper:'Near front leg (upper)',armL_lower:'Near front leg (lower)',handL:'Near front paw',armR_upper:'Far front leg (upper)',armR_lower:'Far front leg (lower)',handR:'Far front paw',legL_upper:'Near hind leg (upper)',legL_lower:'Near hind leg (lower)',shoeL:'Near hind paw',legR_upper:'Far hind leg (upper)',legR_lower:'Far hind leg (lower)',shoeR:'Far hind paw',head_blink:'Head (blink)',mouth_closed:'Muzzle (closed)',mouth_open:'Muzzle (open)',head_sad:'Head (sad)',head_angry:'Head (angry)',head_shock:'Head (startled)',head_laugh:'Head (happy)',mouth_hold:'Muzzle (holding grip)',handL_fist:'Near paw (curled)',handR_fist:'Far paw (curled)',handL_point:'Near paw (raised)',handR_point:'Far paw (raised)',handL_thumb:'Near paw (extended)',handR_thumb:'Far paw (extended)',handL_palm:'Near open paw',handR_palm:'Far open paw'};
    return labels[k] || SHEET_LABEL[k] || k;
  }
  if(type === 'bird'){
    const labels={head:'Head / beak',torso:'Body',tail:'Tail feathers',armL_upper:'Near wing (upper)',armL_lower:'Near wing (lower)',handL:'Near wingtip',armR_upper:'Far wing (upper)',armR_lower:'Far wing (lower)',handR:'Far wingtip',legL_upper:'Near leg (upper)',legL_lower:'Near leg (lower)',shoeL:'Near bird foot',legR_upper:'Far leg (upper)',legR_lower:'Far leg (lower)',shoeR:'Far bird foot',head_blink:'Head (blink)',mouth_closed:'Beak (closed)',mouth_open:'Beak (open)',head_sad:'Head (sad)',head_angry:'Head (alert)',head_shock:'Head (startled)',head_laugh:'Head (happy)',mouth_hold:'Beak (holding grip)',handL_fist:'Near wingtip (folded)',handR_fist:'Far wingtip (folded)',handL_point:'Near wingtip (extended)',handR_point:'Far wingtip (extended)',handL_thumb:'Near wingtip (raised)',handR_thumb:'Far wingtip (raised)',handL_palm:'Near wingtip (fan)',handR_palm:'Far wingtip (fan)'};
    return labels[k] || SHEET_LABEL[k] || k;
  }
  return (ED.view === 'side' && SHEET_LABEL_SIDE[k]) || SHEET_LABEL[k];
}
const SHEET_FILL = 0.68;      // fraction of the stage height a new model fills (leaves headroom for jumps)
// Alternate textures ("expression swaps"). SHEET_ALTS[base] lists every texture that can stand in for `base`: the blink and
// the expression heads replace the head, the open mouth replaces the closed one, and the fist / pointing / thumbs-up /
// open-palm textures replace the plain hand (NOT the forearm -- the forearm is untouched by a gesture swap now that it
// is its own piece). SHEET_ALT_OF is the reverse lookup, used to skip an alt when iterating parts normally (it never
// gets its own independent slot in the draw order -- it stands in for its base).
const SHEET_ALTS = {
  head:['head_blink','head_open','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'],
  mouth_closed:['mouth_open','mouth_hold'],
  handL:['handL_fist','handL_point','handL_thumb','handL_palm','handL_hold'],
  handR:['handR_fist','handR_point','handR_thumb','handR_palm','handR_hold']
};
const SHEET_ALT_OF = {};
Object.keys(SHEET_ALTS).forEach(b=>SHEET_ALTS[b].forEach(a=>{ SHEET_ALT_OF[a] = b; }));
// The expression heads are complete heads (their own mouth included), so the closed/open mouth pieces are not drawn over them.
const SHEET_EMO_HEADS = ['head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'];
// Given a base key and a character's expression state, returns which key should actually be drawn.
// `parts` is whatever object holds the sheet's known part keys (sh.parts at runtime, ED.arts while
// editing) -- if the alt texture isn't actually part of this particular model, always falls back to
// the base so an expression flag left set on an old/basic model can never blank out a part.
function sheetActiveKey(baseKey, expr, parts){
  if(!expr || !parts) return baseKey;
  if(baseKey === 'head'){
    const emo = expr.emo ? 'head_'+expr.emo : null;
    // Keep expression heads intact; blinking is composited as an eye-only overlay for moods.
    if(expr.eyeState==='closed' && !(emo && parts[emo])) return parts.head_blink ? 'head_blink' : baseKey;
    if(expr.eyeState==='open' && !(emo && parts[emo]) && parts.head_open) return 'head_open';
    if((expr.blink || expr.ambientBlink) && !(emo && parts[emo])) return parts.head_blink ? 'head_blink' : baseKey;
    return emo && parts[emo] ? emo : baseKey;
  }
  if(baseKey === 'mouth_closed') {
    if(expr.mouth === 'open' && parts.mouth_open) return 'mouth_open';
    if(expr.mouth === 'hold' && parts.mouth_hold) return 'mouth_hold';
    return baseKey;
  }
  const side = baseKey === 'handL' ? 'L' : baseKey === 'handR' ? 'R' : '';
  if(side){
    if((side === 'L' ? expr.fistL : expr.fistR) && parts['hand'+side+'_fist']) return 'hand'+side+'_fist';
    const g = side === 'L' ? expr.gestL : expr.gestR;
    if(g && parts['hand'+side+'_'+g]) return 'hand'+side+'_'+g;
  }
  return baseKey;
}

// Fresh per-character expression state (blink / mouth / fists). `ambientBlink` is the random idle blink; it is
// kept separate from `blink` (driven by the Talk + Blink clip) so the two can never fight or leave the eyes stuck.
function sheetExprFresh(){ return { blink:false, mouth:'closed', eyeState:'auto', fistL:false, fistR:false, ambientBlink:false, emo:null, gestL:null, gestR:null, customExpressionId:null, customGestureLId:null, customGestureRId:null }; }

Keep edges crisp with no background-colored halo. If your tool truly cannot export transparency, use a pure solid BLACK background instead, and draw the character with NO black or near-black outlines or clothing, so the black can be removed cleanly.";

let twoSheetUploadState = { base:null, variants:null, baseName:'Model', type:'humanoid' };
function twoSheetUi(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
function twoSheetTypeName(type){ return type==='animal'?'Animal':type==='bird'?'Bird':'Humanoid'; }
function updateTwoSheetLabels(){
  const type=twoSheetUploadState.type||activeSheetType||'humanoid', name=twoSheetTypeName(type), species=type!=='humanoid';
  const title=twoSheetUi('autoRigUploadTitle');
  const baseTitle=document.querySelector('#baseSheetUploadArea')?.closest('.two-sheet-card')?.querySelector('.two-sheet-card-title');
  const variantTitle=document.querySelector('#variantSheetUploadArea')?.closest('.two-sheet-card')?.querySelector('.two-sheet-card-title');
  const create=twoSheetUi('twoSheetCreateBtn');
  if(title)title.textContent='Create a two-sheet '+name+' model';
  if(baseTitle)baseTitle.textContent=type==='bird'?'Base Body & Wings':type==='animal'?'Base Animal Body':'Base Character Body';
  if(variantTitle)variantTitle.textContent=species?'Face, Mouth & Expression Variations':'Face & Gesture Variations';
  const bl=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadLabel'),bh=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadHint'),vl=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadLabel'),vh=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadHint');
  if(bl&&!twoSheetUploadState.base)bl.textContent='Upload '+(species?'15-part '+type+' base sheet':'14-part base sheet');
  if(bh&&!twoSheetUploadState.base)bh.textContent=(species?'15 separate anatomy pieces including one independent tail':'14 separate anatomy pieces')+' · transparent PNG';
  if(vl&&!twoSheetUploadState.variants)vl.textContent=twoSheetUploadState.base?(species?'Upload 9-part face-expression sheet':'Upload 16-part expression sheet'):'Upload the base sheet first';
  if(vh&&!twoSheetUploadState.variants)vh.textContent=species?'9 isolated eye, mouth and emotion pieces':'16 isolated face and hand-gesture pieces';
  if(create)create.textContent='Create rigged '+name.toLowerCase();
  document.querySelectorAll('.ev-sheet-copy-sq').forEach(b=>{
    const t='Copy '+type+(b.dataset.copy==='variant'?' expression prompt':' base-sheet prompt');
    b.title=t;b.setAttribute('aria-label',t);
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.ev-sheet-type-btn').forEach(btn=>{
    const active=btn.dataset.sheetType===type;btn.classList.toggle('active',active);btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
  });
}
function twoSheetResetPreview(prefix){
  const preview=twoSheetUi(prefix+'PreviewImg'), icon=twoSheetUi(prefix+'UploadIcon');
  const area=twoSheetUi(prefix+'UploadArea');
  if(preview){ if(preview.src && preview.src.startsWith('blob:')) URL.revokeObjectURL(preview.src); preview.removeAttribute('src'); preview.style.display='none'; }
  if(icon) icon.style.display='';
  if(area) area.classList.remove('ready');
}
function updateTwoSheetStatus(){
  const baseOK=!!twoSheetUploadState.base, variantOK=!!twoSheetUploadState.variants;
  const type=twoSheetUploadState.type||'humanoid', species=type!=='humanoid';
  const b=twoSheetUi('baseSheetStatus'), v=twoSheetUi('variantSheetStatus'), btn=twoSheetUi('twoSheetCreateBtn');
  if(b){ const count=baseOK?Object.keys(twoSheetUploadState.base||{}).length:(species?15:14); b.textContent=baseOK?count+' '+(species?type+' anatomy':'base-body')+' parts detected and ready.':''; b.classList.toggle('ready',baseOK); }
  if(v){ v.textContent=variantOK?(species?'9 face and mouth pieces':'Expression and hand-gesture sheet')+' accepted and ready.':''; v.classList.toggle('ready',variantOK); }
  if(btn) btn.disabled=!(baseOK&&variantOK);
  updateTwoSheetLabels();
}
function resetTwoSheetUploadUI(){
  const type=activeSheetType||'humanoid';
  twoSheetUploadState={base:null,variants:null,baseName:'Model',type};
  twoSheetResetPreview('baseSheet'); twoSheetResetPreview('variantSheet');
  const b=twoSheetUi('baseSheetStatus'),v=twoSheetUi('variantSheetStatus');if(b){b.textContent='';b.classList.remove('ready');}if(v){v.textContent='';v.classList.remove('ready');}
  updateTwoSheetLabels();updateTwoSheetStatus();
}
function loadImageFile(file, onLoaded, onError){
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(file), img=new Image();
  img.onload=()=>onLoaded(img,url);
  img.onerror=()=>{ URL.revokeObjectURL(url); if(onError) onError(); };
  img.src=url;
}
function handleBaseSheetFile(input){
  const file=input.files&&input.files[0]; input.value=''; if(!file) return;
  const type=twoSheetUploadState.type||activeSheetType||'humanoid', species=type!=='humanoid';
  loadImageFile(file,(img,url)=>{
    let parsed;
    try{ parsed=sheetParse(img,type); }catch(err){ console.error(err); showToast('Could not analyze the '+(species?'15-part ':'14-part ')+type+' base sheet',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2400}); URL.revokeObjectURL(url); return; }
    const expectedBaseKeys=parsed.layout==='species15'?SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_15:SHEET_KEYS_14;
    if(parsed.error || !parsed.arts || !expectedBaseKeys.every(k=>parsed.arts[k])){
      showToast(parsed.error||(species?'The new animal/bird base sheet must contain exactly 15 separate pieces, including one independent tail.':'The base sheet must contain exactly 14 separate anatomy pieces.'),{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3000});
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url); return;
    }
    twoSheetUploadState.base=parsed.arts;twoSheetUploadState.variants=null;
    twoSheetUploadState.baseName=(file.name||'Model').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').slice(0,24)||'Model';
    if(!species){
      if(twoSheetUploadState.base.torso)twoSheetUploadState.base.torso.bodyIncludesNeckAndWaist=true;
      if(twoSheetUploadState.base.head)twoSheetUploadState.base.head.recoveredHeadWithoutNeck=true;
    }
    const preview=twoSheetUi('baseSheetPreviewImg'),icon=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadIcon'),area=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadArea');
    if(preview){preview.src=url;preview.style.display='block';}if(icon)icon.style.display='none';if(area)area.classList.add('ready');
    const label=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadLabel'),hint=twoSheetUi('baseSheetUploadHint');
    if(label)label.textContent='Base sheet ready';if(hint)hint.textContent=Object.keys(parsed.arts).length+' anatomy pieces detected'+(parsed.arts.tail?' · independent tail ready':' · legacy sheet without separate tail')+' · tap to replace';
    twoSheetResetPreview('variantSheet');
    updateTwoSheetStatus();
    showToast(Object.keys(parsed.arts).length+'-part '+type+' base sheet accepted. Now upload the matching face-expression sheet.',{duration:2300});
  },()=>showToast('Could not open that base-body image',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800}));
}
function parseVariantSheetImage(img,type){
  if(type==='animal'||type==='bird'){
    const species=sheetParse(img,'species-variants');
    if(!species.error&&species.layout==='speciesVariants9'&&SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_VARIANTS.every(k=>species.arts&&species.arts[k]))return {arts:species.arts,layout:'speciesVariants9'};
    return {error:species.error||'The animal/bird expression sheet must contain exactly 9 isolated face and mouth pieces in a 3×3 layout.'};
  }
  const compact=sheetParse(img,'humanoid-variants');
  if(!compact.error && (compact.layout==='variants16' || compact.layout==='variants13')) return {arts:compact.arts,layout:compact.layout};
  const legacy=sheetParse(img,'humanoid');
  if(!legacy.error && legacy.layout==='legacy27'){
    const extras={};Object.keys(legacy.arts||{}).forEach(k=>{if(!SHEET_KEYS_12.includes(k)&&!SHEET_KEYS_14.includes(k)&&legacy.arts[k])extras[k]=legacy.arts[k];});
    if(Object.keys(extras).length>=13)return {arts:extras,layout:'legacy27-variants'};
  }
  return {error:compact.error||legacy.error||'The new second sheet must contain 16 face and hand-gesture pieces. Legacy 13-part and complete 27-part sheets are still supported.'};
}
function handleVariantSheetFile(input){
  const file=input.files&&input.files[0];input.value='';if(!file)return;
  const type=twoSheetUploadState.type||activeSheetType||'humanoid',species=type!=='humanoid';
  if(!twoSheetUploadState.base){showToast('Upload the animal/bird base sheet first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2000});return;}
  loadImageFile(file,(img,url)=>{
    let parsed;try{parsed=parseVariantSheetImage(img,type);}catch(err){console.error(err);showToast('Could not analyze the expression sheet',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2400});URL.revokeObjectURL(url);return;}
    const keys=species?SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_VARIANTS:(parsed.layout==='variants13'?SHEET_KEYS_VARIANTS_13:SHEET_KEYS_VARIANTS_16);
    const hasFaceParts=!!parsed.arts&&Object.keys(parsed.arts).some(k=>['mouth_closed','mouth_open','mouth_hold','head_blink','head_sad','head_angry','head_shock','head_laugh'].includes(k));
    const missingCompactPart=(parsed.layout==='speciesVariants9'||parsed.layout==='variants16'||parsed.layout==='variants13')&&(!parsed.arts||!keys.every(k=>parsed.arts[k]));
    if(parsed.error||!parsed.arts||!hasFaceParts||missingCompactPart){
      showToast(parsed.error||(species?'The second sheet must contain all 9 face and mouth pieces.':'The second sheet must contain all 16 expression and hand-gesture pieces.'),{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:3000});URL.revokeObjectURL(url);return;
    }
    twoSheetUploadState.variants=parsed.arts;
    const preview=twoSheetUi('variantSheetPreviewImg'),icon=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadIcon'),area=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadArea');
    if(preview){preview.src=url;preview.style.display='block';}if(icon)icon.style.display='none';if(area)area.classList.add('ready');
    const label=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadLabel'),hint=twoSheetUi('variantSheetUploadHint');
    if(label)label.textContent='Expression sheet ready';if(hint)hint.textContent=Object.keys(parsed.arts).length+' supplemental face pieces detected · tap to replace';
    updateTwoSheetStatus();showToast(species?'Animal/bird face-expression sheet accepted.':'Expression and gesture sheet accepted.',{duration:1600});
  },()=>showToast('Could not open that expression sheet',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800}));
}
function createFromTwoSheets(){
  if(!twoSheetUploadState.base||!twoSheetUploadState.variants){showToast('Upload both model sheets first',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});return;}
  const type=twoSheetUploadState.type||activeSheetType||'humanoid',species=type!=='humanoid';
  const arts={...twoSheetUploadState.base,...twoSheetUploadState.variants};
  if(!species){if(arts.torso)arts.torso.bodyIncludesNeckAndWaist=true;if(arts.head)arts.head.recoveredHeadWithoutNeck=true;}
  const baseKeys=species&&SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_15.every(k=>arts[k])?SHEET_KEYS_SPECIES_15:SHEET_KEYS_14;
  const missing=baseKeys.filter(k=>!arts[k]);
  if(missing.length){showToast('The base sheet is missing one or more required anatomy pieces. Please re-upload it.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2600});return;}
  const name=twoSheetUploadState.baseName||twoSheetTypeName(type),kind=sheetCreateMode==='newAsset'?'newAsset':'new';
  const view=species?'side':'front',order=(species?SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER_SIDE:SHEET_DEFAULT_ORDER).filter(k=>arts[k]);
  closeAutoRigUploader();
  edOpen({kind,view,modelType:type,arts,st:edDefaultLayout(arts,view,type),order,name});
}

document.getElementById('baseSheetFileInput').addEventListener('change',e=>handleBaseSheetFile(e.target));
document.getElementById('variantSheetFileInput').addEventListener('change',e=>handleVariantSheetFile(e.target));

  const t=type==='bird'?'bird':'animal';
  copyTextPrompt(SPECIES_PROMPTS[t],t==='bird'?'Bird 15-part base-sheet prompt':'Animal 15-part base-sheet prompt');
}
function copySpeciesVariantPrompt(type){
  const t=type==='bird'?'bird':'animal';
  copyTextPrompt(SPECIES_VARIANT_PROMPTS[t],t==='bird'?'Bird 9-part face-expression prompt':'Animal 9-part face-expression prompt');
}
function copyActiveBaseSheetPrompt(){
  if(activeSheetType==='animal'||activeSheetType==='bird')return copySpeciesPrompt(activeSheetType);
  return copyBody14Prompt();
}
function copyActiveVariantSheetPrompt(){
  if(activeSheetType==='animal'||activeSheetType==='bird')return copySpeciesVariantPrompt(activeSheetType);
  return copyVariants16Prompt();
}
function copyActiveSheetPrompt(){return copyActiveBaseSheetPrompt();}
function evCopySheetPrompt(btn,which){
  /* base -> species 15-part / humanoid 14-part body prompt, variant -> face/expression prompt, for the selected model type */
  if(which==='variant')copyActiveVariantSheetPrompt();else copyActiveBaseSheetPrompt();
  if(!btn)return;
  btn.classList.add('copied');
  clearTimeout(btn._evCopyT);
  btn._evCopyT=setTimeout(function(){btn.classList.remove('copied');},1400);
}
function setUploadSheetType(type){
  activeSheetType=['animal','bird'].includes(type)?type:'humanoid';
  if(typeof twoSheetUploadState==='object'&&twoSheetUploadState){
    twoSheetUploadState.type=activeSheetType;
    if(typeof resetTwoSheetUploadUI==='function')resetTwoSheetUploadUI();
    else if(typeof updateTwoSheetLabels==='function')updateTwoSheetLabels();
  }
  if(typeof autoRigState!=='undefined'&&autoRigState)autoRigState.modelType=activeSheetType;
  const title=document.getElementById('autoRigUploadTitle'),sub=document.getElementById('sheetUploadSub'),copy=document.getElementById('sheetCopyExtBtn'),label=document.getElementById('autoRigUploadLabel'),hint=document.getElementById('autoRigUploadHint');
  if(title)title.textContent='Create a two-sheet '+twoSheetTypeName(activeSheetType)+' model';
  if(sub)sub.textContent='Legacy combined-sheet imports remain available for existing projects.';
  if(copy)copy.textContent='Copy legacy 27-part humanoid prompt';
  if(label&&label.textContent!=='Analyzing…')label.textContent='Legacy combined sheet upload';
  if(hint)hint.textContent='Legacy transparent PNG import';
  document.querySelectorAll('.ev-sheet-type-btn').forEach(btn=>{const active=btn.dataset.sheetType===activeSheetType;btn.classList.toggle('active',active);btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');});
  if(typeof updateTwoSheetLabels==='function')updateTwoSheetLabels();
}

function copyTextPrompt(text, label){
  const done = ()=>showToast(label+' copied. Paste it into your image AI', {icon:toastIcons.check, duration:2200});
  function fb(){ const t = document.createElement('textarea'); t.value = text; document.body.appendChild(t); t.select(); try{ document.execCommand('copy'); done(); }catch(e){ showToast('Copy failed'); } t.remove(); }
  if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, fb); else fb();
}
function copySheetPrompt(){ copyTextPrompt(SHEET_PROMPT_TURNED_EXT, '27-part humanoid model-sheet prompt'); }
function copySheetPromptExt(){ copySheetPrompt(); }
function copyBody14Prompt(){ copyTextPrompt(SHEET_PROMPT_TWO_BODY,'14-part base-body sheet prompt'); }
function copyVariants16Prompt(){ copyTextPrompt(SHEET_PROMPT_VARIANTS_16,'16-part expressions and gestures prompt'); }
function copyVariants13Prompt(){ return copyVariants16Prompt(); }

/* ---------- 1) parse the sheet ---------- */
document.getElementById('sheetFileInput').addEventListener('change', e=>{
  const f = e.target.files[0]; e.target.value = ''; if(!f) return;
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
  document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').src = url;
  document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('autoRigUploadLabel').textContent = 'Analyzing…';
  const resetTile = ()=>{
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadPreviewImg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('autoRigUploadIcon').style.display = '';
    setUploadSheetType(activeSheetType);
  };
  const img = new Image();
  img.onload = ()=>{ try{ buildFromSheet(img, f.name, activeSheetType); }catch(err){ console.error(err); showToast("Couldn't read this sheet", {icon:toastIcons.info, duration:2400}); resetTile(); } };
  img.onerror = ()=>{ showToast("Couldn't open that image", {icon:toastIcons.info}); resetTile(); };
  img.src = url;
});
// Remove dark contour/shadow pixels only on the small end zones that are meant to be hidden at a joint.
// The outer silhouette elsewhere (faces, hair, clothes and shoe soles) is untouched. We recolour contour
// pixels instead of erasing alpha, so antialiased edges stay closed when parts overlap.
function edCleanJointEdge(canvas, key, modelType){
  if(!canvas || !canvas.width || !canvas.height) return;
  // Preserve original fur, scales, feathers, beaks and claws; humanoid skin/fabric cleanup is not applied here.
  if(modelType === 'animal' || modelType === 'bird') return null;
  const w=canvas.width, h=canvas.height, ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  let im; try{ im=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h); }catch(e){ return; }
  const d=im.data, original=new Uint8ClampedArray(d), isHead=/^head(?:_|$)/.test(key), torso=key==='torso';
  const isMouth=key==='mouth_closed'||key==='mouth_open'||key==='mouth_hold';
  // Mouth pieces are overlays on top of the full head. Remove dark pixels only within a few pixels
  // of the outer alpha silhouette, recolouring them to the same skin palette; internal lip/smile
  // details remain untouched because they are surrounded by opaque mouth/skin pixels.
  if(isMouth){
    const skinSamples=[[],[],[]];
    for(let p=0;p<w*h;p++){
      const i=p*4, a=original[i+3]; if(a<150) continue;
      const r=original[i],g=original[i+1],b=original[i+2];
      if(r>165 && g>75 && b>30 && r>g*1.12 && g>b*1.12 && r-g>28 && g-b>12){
        skinSamples[0].push(r); skinSamples[1].push(g); skinSamples[2].push(b);
      }
    }
    const med=a=>{ if(!a.length) return null; a.sort((x,y)=>x-y); return a[Math.floor(a.length/2)]; };
    const skin=skinSamples.map(med);
    if(skin.every(v=>v!==null)){
      for(let y=0;y<h;y++) for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const i=(y*w+x)*4; if(original[i+3]<35) continue;
        const lum=.2126*original[i]+.7152*original[i+1]+.0722*original[i+2];
        if(lum>220) continue;
        let nearOutside=false;
        for(let oy=-5;oy<=5&&!nearOutside;oy++) for(let ox=-5;ox<=5;ox++){
          if(ox*ox+oy*oy>64) continue;
          const nx=x+ox, ny=y+oy;
          if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=w||ny>=h||original[(ny*w+nx)*4+3]<28){ nearOutside=true; break; }
        }
        if(!nearOutside) continue;
        // Preserve edge alpha for anti-aliasing while removing its dark RGB fringe.
        const blend=1;
        for(let c=0;c<3;c++) d[i+c]=Math.round(original[i+c]*(1-blend)+skin[c]*blend);
      }
    }
    ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);
    return null;
  }
  const point=/^hand[LR]_point$/.test(key), thumbOrPalm=/^hand[LR]_(?:thumb|palm)$/.test(key);
  const hand=/^hand[LR](?:_fist)?$/.test(key), upperArm=/^arm[LR]_upper$/.test(key), lowerArm=/^arm[LR]_lower$/.test(key);
  const upperLeg=/^leg[LR]_upper$/.test(key), lowerLeg=/^leg[LR]_lower$/.test(key);
  const inJointBand=(x,y)=>{
    // Keep the jaw/chin silhouette intact. A recovered head ends at the face/jaw boundary,
    // not around the neck, so only the final few pixels are a hidden overlap zone.
    if(isHead) return y>=h*.97 && x>=w*.30 && x<=w*.70;
    if(torso){
      // The bottom of this Body image may contain the REAL dark-blue trouser/waist piece from the
      // uploaded sheet. It is artwork, not a hidden joint seam: never recolour/flatten the bottom
      // centre band, or the denim triangle can turn shirt-blue and look like an added blue patch.
      // Only clean the tiny shoulder/arm overlap corners; preserve the complete lower hem and
      // every pants-start pixel with its original RGB and alpha values.
      if(y>=h*.05 && y<=h*.27 && ((x<=w*.14)||(x>=w*.86))) return true;
      return false;
    }
    if(point){ return (key==='handL_point' ? x<=w*.10 : x>=w*.90) && y>=h*.22 && y<=h*.78; }
    if(thumbOrPalm) return y>=h*.89 && x>=w*.18 && x<=w*.82;
    if(hand) return y<=h*.20 && x>=w*.14 && x<=w*.86;
    if(upperArm || lowerArm || upperLeg) return (y<=h*.14 || y>=h*.86) && x>=w*.08 && x<=w*.92;
    // Shin/foot artwork keeps its shoe outline; only the knee end is a joining edge.
    if(lowerLeg) return y<=h*.15 && x>=w*.08 && x<=w*.92;
    return false;
  };
  const lum=(i)=>.2126*original[i]+.7152*original[i+1]+.0722*original[i+2];
  for(let y=1;y<h-1;y++) for(let x=1;x<w-1;x++){
    const i=(y*w+x)*4; if(original[i+3]<80 || !inJointBand(x,y)) continue;
    const l=lum(i); if(l>185) continue;
    let rr=0,gg=0,bb=0,n=0,maxL=0;
    for(let oy=-1;oy<=1;oy++) for(let ox=-1;ox<=1;ox++){
      if(!ox&&!oy) continue; const j=((y+oy)*w+x+ox)*4; if(original[j+3]<90) continue;
      const lj=lum(j); if(lj>maxL) maxL=lj;
      if(lj>l+4){ rr+=original[j]; gg+=original[j+1]; bb+=original[j+2]; n++; }
    }
    // Only soften a dark pixel that is clearly a contour against a lighter interior fill.
    if(n && maxL>l+6 && l < maxL*.97){ d[i]=Math.round(rr/n); d[i+1]=Math.round(gg/n); d[i+2]=Math.round(bb/n); }
  }
  // Flatten mild skin/fabric shading in the joint overlap itself. Several generated sheets draw a
  // brown/black wrist crease or dark cuff-shadow a few pixels INSIDE the crop, not just on its edge,
  // so edge-only decontouring cannot remove it. Sample each material from the interior and reuse its
  // own median colour just in those tiny hidden zones; silhouette alpha and all non-joint pixels stay.
  const samples={skin:[[],[],[]],blue:[[],[],[]],gray:[[],[],[]],other:[[],[],[]]};
  for(let y=2;y<h-2;y++) for(let x=2;x<w-2;x++){
    const i=(y*w+x)*4; if(original[i+3]<150 || inJointBand(x,y)) continue;
    const r=original[i],gg=original[i+1],b=original[i+2],mx=Math.max(r,gg,b),mn=Math.min(r,gg,b);
    let cls=null;
    if(r>135 && gg>70 && r>gg*1.12 && gg>b*1.12 && r-b>28) cls='skin';
    else if(b>r*1.08 && b>gg*1.02 && b-gg>12) cls='blue';
    else if(mx<135 && mx-mn<55) cls='gray';
    else cls='other';
    if(cls) for(let c=0;c<3;c++) samples[cls][c].push(original[i+c]);
  }
  const median=arr=>{ if(arr.length<12) return null; arr.sort((a,b)=>a-b); return arr[Math.floor(arr.length/2)]; };
  const palette={}; Object.keys(samples).forEach(k=>{ palette[k]=samples[k].map(median); if(palette[k].some(v=>v===null)) palette[k]=null; });
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++) for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    if(!inJointBand(x,y)) continue; const i=(y*w+x)*4; if(original[i+3]<45) continue;
    const r=original[i],gg=original[i+1],b=original[i+2],mx=Math.max(r,gg,b),mn=Math.min(r,gg,b); let cls=null;
    if(r>125 && gg>65 && r>gg*1.10 && gg>b*1.10 && r-b>28) cls='skin';
    else if(b>r*1.06 && b>gg*.99 && b-gg>8) cls='blue';
    else if(mx<140 && mx-mn<62) cls='gray';
    const col=cls&&palette[cls]; if(!col) continue;
    for(let c=0;c<3;c++) d[i+c]=Math.round(original[i+c]*.08+col[c]*.92);
  }
  ctx.putImageData(im,0,0);
  return palette;
}
// One cropped part -> "art": pixels + hit mask + default pivot (the joint it rotates around) + a few measurements.
function edMakeArt(canvas, key, modelType){
  const jointColors = edCleanJointEdge(canvas,key,modelType);
  const w = canvas.width, h = canvas.height, d = canvas.getContext('2d').getImageData(0,0,w,h).data, alpha = new Uint8Array(w*h);
  for(let i=0;i<w*h;i++) alpha[i] = d[i*4+3] > 30 ? 1 : 0;
  const cx = (a,b)=>{ let s = 0, n = 0; for(let y = Math.floor(a); y < Math.min(h, Math.ceil(b)); y++) for(let x = 0; x < w; x++) if(alpha[y*w+x]){ s += x; n++; } return n ? s/n : w/2; };
  let skinTop = -1;                         // first row that is mostly skin-coloured (where a sleeve ends and the bare forearm begins)
  for(let y = 0; y < h && skinTop < 0; y++){ let sk = 0, op = 0; for(let x = 0; x < w; x++){ const i = (y*w+x)*4; if(d[i+3] > 30){ op++; const r = d[i], g = d[i+1], b = d[i+2]; if(r > 120 && r > g && g > b && r-b > 35) sk++; } } if(op > 3 && sk/op > 0.5) skinTop = y; }
  let dpx, dpy;
  if(/^head/.test(key)){ dpx = cx(h*0.88, h); dpy = h*0.97; }
  else if(key === 'torso'){ dpx = w/2; dpy = h*0.9; }
  else if(key === 'tail' && (modelType === 'animal' || modelType === 'bird')){ dpx = w*0.94; dpy = h*0.5; }
  else if(key === 'mouth_closed' || key === 'mouth_open' || key === 'mouth_hold'){ dpx = w/2; dpy = h/2; }
  // Animal paw and bird wingtip alternatives all share a top-edge attachment point. The humanoid
  // point/thumb/palm pivots below are intentionally kept for human hand silhouettes only.
  else if((modelType === 'animal' || modelType === 'bird') && /^hand[LR](?:_(?:fist|point|thumb|palm))?$/.test(key)){ dpx = cx(0,h*0.1); dpy = h*0.06; }
  // Gesture art is not always upright: pointing hands have a wrist on the side, while thumbs-up
  // and palm pieces have the wrist at the bottom. Put the pivot on the actual wrist edge instead
  // of treating every variant like the plain open hand (this prevents detached gesture swaps).
  else if(/^handL_point$/.test(key)){ dpx = w*0.035; dpy = h*0.5; }
  else if(/^handR_point$/.test(key)){ dpx = w*0.965; dpy = h*0.5; }
  else if(/^hand[LR]_(?:thumb|palm)$/.test(key)){ dpx = cx(h*0.88,h); dpy = h*0.96; }
  else if(/^hand[LR](?:_fist)?$/.test(key)){ dpx = cx(0,h*0.1); dpy = h*0.06; }
  else { dpx = cx(0, h*0.1); dpy = h*(/lower|fist|point|thumb|palm/.test(key) ? 0.06 : 0.05); }
  return { canvas, w, h, alpha, dpx, dpy, jointColors:jointColors||{}, botCx:cx(h*0.92, h), skinTop: skinTop < 0 ? -1 : skinTop/h };
}
// The new humanoid format places cut-outs around an upright anatomical silhouette (head above
// torso, arms at the sides, legs below) instead of putting every piece into a rectangular grid.
// This detector keeps legacy two-row 12-piece sheets usable by checking that the topmost piece is
// near the horizontal centre and that a separate central torso-like component sits below it.
function sheetLooksLikeRegularPartGrid(parts, w, h){
  if(!parts || parts.length < 10 || !w || !h) return false;
  const bucket = (values, tol) => {
    const out=[];
    values.slice().sort((a,b)=>a-b).forEach(v=>{
      const last=out[out.length-1];
      if(!last || Math.abs(v-last.mean)>tol) out.push({values:[v],mean:v,count:1});
      else { last.values.push(v); last.mean=last.values.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/last.values.length; last.count=last.values.length; }
    });
    return out;
  };
  const xs=parts.map(b=>(b.x0+b.x1)/2), ys=parts.map(b=>(b.y0+b.y1)/2);
  const cols=bucket(xs,Math.max(3,w*.055)), rows=bucket(ys,Math.max(3,h*.035));
  if(cols.length<4 || rows.length<3) return false;
  const gapStats=clusters=>{
    const gaps=[]; for(let i=1;i<clusters.length;i++) gaps.push(clusters[i].mean-clusters[i-1].mean);
    const mean=gaps.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/Math.max(1,gaps.length);
    return {mean,spread:mean?Math.max(...gaps.map(v=>Math.abs(v-mean)))/mean:Infinity,min:Math.min(...gaps)};
  };
  const gx=gapStats(cols), gy=gapStats(rows);
  // A true model-sheet grid repeats cell-centre spacing horizontally AND vertically.
  // Anatomical cut-outs may form rows, but their irregular shoulder/hand/leg x positions
  // must not be mistaken for a grid simply because some centres happen to line up.
  if(gx.spread>.38 || gy.spread>.48 || gx.min<w*.075 || gy.min<h*.035) return false;
  const repeatedCols=cols.filter(c=>c.count>=2).length;
  const repeatedRows=rows.filter(r=>r.count>=3).length;
  const strongRows=rows.filter(r=>r.count>=Math.max(2,Math.ceil(cols.length*.55))).length;
  const strongCols=cols.filter(c=>c.count>=2).length;
  return repeatedCols>=Math.ceil(cols.length*.65) && repeatedRows>=2 && strongRows>=Math.ceil(rows.length*.6) && strongCols>=Math.ceil(cols.length*.7) && parts.length/cols.length>=1.8;
}
function sheetIsAnatomy12(parts, w, h){
  if(!parts || parts.length !== 12) return false;
  const cx = b => (b.x0 + b.x1) / 2;
  const cy = b => (b.y0 + b.y1) / 2;
  const head = parts.reduce((best,b)=>cy(b)<cy(best)?b:best, parts[0]);
  const headX = cx(head) / Math.max(1,w);
  if(headX < 0.30 || headX > 0.70) return false;
  const possible = parts.filter(b=>b!==head && cx(b)/Math.max(1,w)>=0.31 && cx(b)/Math.max(1,w)<=0.69 && cy(b)>cy(head)+h*0.06 && cy(b)<h*0.67);
  if(!possible.length) return false;
  const torso = possible.sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n)[0];
  const torsoX = cx(torso) / Math.max(1,w), torsoY = cy(torso) / Math.max(1,h), headY = cy(head) / Math.max(1,h);
  return torsoX >= 0.32 && torsoX <= 0.68 && torsoY > headY + 0.06 && torsoY < 0.68;
}
function sheetOrderAnatomy12(parts, w, h){
  const cx = b => (b.x0 + b.x1) / 2;
  const cy = b => (b.y0 + b.y1) / 2;
  const centreX = w / 2;
  const list = parts.slice();
  const head = list.reduce((best,b)=>cy(b)<cy(best)?b:best, list[0]);
  let rest = list.filter(b=>b!==head);
  const torsoPool = rest.filter(b=>cx(b) >= w*0.31 && cx(b) <= w*0.69 && cy(b) > cy(head)+h*0.05 && cy(b) < h*0.68);
  if(!torsoPool.length) return null;
  const torso = torsoPool.sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n)[0];
  rest = rest.filter(b=>b!==torso);
  // Legs sit lower AND closer to the canvas centre than the side-positioned arms/hands.
  // Combining both signals is safer than relying on vertical position alone, because a relaxed
  // hand can hang down to hip height in an anatomy-position sheet.
  const legParts = rest.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const score = item => Math.abs(cx(item)-centreX)/Math.max(1,w)*1.2 - (cy(item)/Math.max(1,h))*0.4;
    return score(a)-score(b);
  }).slice(0,4);
  const armParts = rest.filter(b=>!legParts.includes(b));
  if(armParts.length !== 6 || legParts.length !== 4) return null;
  const byY = items => items.slice().sort((a,b)=>cy(a)-cy(b));
  const bySide = pair => pair.slice().sort((a,b)=>cx(a)-cx(b)); // viewer-left, then viewer-right
  const armSorted = byY(armParts);
  const upperArms = bySide(armSorted.slice(0,2));
  const forearms = bySide(armSorted.slice(2,4));
  const hands = bySide(armSorted.slice(4,6));
  const legSorted = byY(legParts);
  const thighs = bySide(legSorted.slice(0,2));
  const shins = bySide(legSorted.slice(2,4));
  if([upperArms,forearms,hands,thighs,shins].some(pair=>pair.length!==2)) return null;
  return [head, torso, upperArms[0], forearms[0], hands[0], upperArms[1], forearms[1], hands[1], thighs[0], shins[0], thighs[1], shins[1]];
}

// Detect a humanoid sheet arranged around one standing figure, rather than in a fixed grid.
// Several image generators let the head/neck/t-shirt edges touch, or let a wrist connect to a hand.
// These helpers use each component's position relative to the whole figure and can split those
// fused regions at the visible anatomical boundary before assigning editor part names.
function sheetFigureMetrics(parts){
  const x0=Math.min(...parts.map(b=>b.x0)), y0=Math.min(...parts.map(b=>b.y0));
  const x1=Math.max(...parts.map(b=>b.x1)), y1=Math.max(...parts.map(b=>b.y1));
  const w=Math.max(1,x1-x0+1), h=Math.max(1,y1-y0+1), mid=x0+w/2;
  const cx=b=>(b.x0+b.x1)/2, cy=b=>(b.y0+b.y1)/2;
  return {x0,y0,x1,y1,w,h,mid,cx,cy,yr:b=>(cy(b)-y0)/h,dx:b=>Math.abs(cx(b)-mid)/w,bw:b=>b.x1-b.x0+1,bh:b=>b.y1-b.y0+1};
}
function sheetOrderAnatomy14(parts,w,h){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==14) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts);
  if(M.h < M.w*1.2) return null;
  const leftOf=(a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b);
  // A genuine anatomy-position sheet has a small top-centre head and a separate, broader torso.
  const headPool=parts.filter(b=>b.y0<=M.y0+M.h*.20 && M.yr(b)<.22 && M.dx(b)<.19 && M.bh(b)<M.h*.34);
  if(!headPool.length) return null;
  const head=headPool.sort((a,b)=>a.y0-b.y0 || b.n-a.n)[0];
  const torsoPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==head && M.yr(b)>.19 && M.yr(b)<.58 && M.dx(b)<.20 && M.bh(b)>M.h*.12);
  if(!torsoPool.length) return null;
  const torso=torsoPool.sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n)[0];
  const used=new Set([head,torso]);
  const upper=parts.filter(b=>!used.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.12 && M.yr(b)<.42 && M.dx(b)>.18).sort(leftOf);
  if(upper.length!==2) return null;
  upper.forEach(b=>used.add(b));
  const shoes=parts.filter(b=>!used.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.89 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.83).sort(leftOf);
  if(shoes.length!==2) return null; shoes.forEach(b=>used.add(b));
  const shins=parts.filter(b=>!used.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.66 && M.yr(b)<.91 && M.dx(b)<.31 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.58).sort(leftOf);
  if(shins.length!==2) return null; shins.forEach(b=>used.add(b));
  const thighs=parts.filter(b=>!used.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.42 && M.yr(b)<.70 && M.dx(b)<.25 && b.y0>M.y0+M.h*.35).sort(leftOf);
  if(thighs.length!==2) return null; thighs.forEach(b=>used.add(b));
  const lower=parts.filter(b=>!used.has(b) && M.yr(b)>.31 && M.yr(b)<.68 && M.dx(b)>.17);
  if(lower.length!==4) return null;
  const l=lower.filter(b=>M.cx(b)<M.mid).sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  const r=lower.filter(b=>M.cx(b)>=M.mid).sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  if(l.length!==2 || r.length!==2) return null;
  // Key order is deliberately the same as SHEET_KEYS_14; L/R means viewer-left/viewer-right.
  return [head,torso,upper[0],upper[1],l[0],l[1],r[0],r[1],thighs[0],thighs[1],shins[0],shins[1],shoes[0],shoes[1]];
}
// Positional fallback for anatomy-position sheets generated with slightly different spacing.
// The six central/lower pieces are ranked into thighs, shins and shoes; the remaining six are
// grouped by viewer-left/viewer-right and ordered from shoulder to hand. This avoids assigning a
// forearm or sleeve to Head/Hand merely because an AI placed it a little lower than the template.
function sheetOrderAnatomy14ByPosition(parts,w,h){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==14) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts);
  if(M.h < M.w*.82) return null;
  const scoreHead=b=>M.yr(b)*1.0 + M.dx(b)*1.15 + Math.max(0,M.bh(b)/M.h-.22)*.15;
  const headPool=parts.filter(b=>M.yr(b)<.36 && M.dx(b)<.28);
  if(!headPool.length) return null;
  const head=headPool.slice().sort((a,b)=>scoreHead(a)-scoreHead(b))[0];
  const torsoPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==head && M.yr(b)>.12 && M.yr(b)<.68 && M.dx(b)<.25 && M.bw(b)>M.bh(b)*.38);
  if(!torsoPool.length) return null;
  const torso=torsoPool.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const sc=x=>Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,x.n))*2 + M.bw(x)*.6 + M.bh(x)*.15 - M.dx(x)*M.w*.8 - Math.abs(M.yr(x)-.43)*M.h*.08;
    return sc(b)-sc(a);
  })[0];
  const rest=parts.filter(b=>b!==head && b!==torso);
  const lower=rest.filter(b=>M.yr(b)>.34);
  if(lower.length<6) return null;
  // Leg parts sit closer to the centre line; the y bias separates them from hands which hang farther out.
  const legScore=b=>M.dx(b) + Math.max(0,.55-M.yr(b))*.42;
  let legs=lower.slice().sort((a,b)=>legScore(a)-legScore(b)).slice(0,6);
  if(legs.length!==6) return null;
  const orderedLegs=legs.slice().sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  const legsLeftToRight=(pair)=>pair.slice().sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  const thighs=legsLeftToRight(orderedLegs.slice(0,2));
  const shins=legsLeftToRight(orderedLegs.slice(2,4));
  const shoes=legsLeftToRight(orderedLegs.slice(4,6));
  const armParts=rest.filter(b=>!legs.includes(b));
  if(armParts.length!==6) return null;
  let left=armParts.filter(b=>M.cx(b)<M.mid), right=armParts.filter(b=>M.cx(b)>=M.mid);
  if(left.length!==3 || right.length!==3) return null;
  left=left.sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b)); right=right.sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  return [head,torso,left[0],right[0],left[1],left[2],right[1],right[2],thighs[0],thighs[1],shins[0],shins[1],shoes[0],shoes[1]];
}

// Positional fallback for older 12-piece anatomical sheets with no separate shoes.
function sheetOrderAnatomy12ByPosition(parts,w,h){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==12) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts);
  if(M.h < M.w*.72) return null;
  const headPool=parts.filter(b=>M.yr(b)<.36 && M.dx(b)<.28);
  if(!headPool.length) return null;
  const head=headPool.slice().sort((a,b)=>(M.yr(a)+M.dx(a)*1.1)-(M.yr(b)+M.dx(b)*1.1))[0];
  const torsoPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==head && M.yr(b)>.12 && M.yr(b)<.67 && M.dx(b)<.25 && M.bw(b)>M.bh(b)*.34);
  if(!torsoPool.length) return null;
  const torso=torsoPool.slice().sort((a,b)=>(Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,b.n))*2+M.bw(b)*.5-M.dx(b)*M.w*.8)-(Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,a.n))*2+M.bw(a)*.5-M.dx(a)*M.w*.8))[0];
  const rest=parts.filter(b=>b!==head && b!==torso), lower=rest.filter(b=>M.yr(b)>.34);
  if(lower.length<4) return null;
  const legScore=b=>M.dx(b)+Math.max(0,.55-M.yr(b))*.42;
  const legs=lower.slice().sort((a,b)=>legScore(a)-legScore(b)).slice(0,4);
  const arms=rest.filter(b=>!legs.includes(b));
  if(arms.length!==6) return null;
  let left=arms.filter(b=>M.cx(b)<M.mid), right=arms.filter(b=>M.cx(b)>=M.mid);
  if(left.length!==3||right.length!==3) return null;
  left.sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b)); right.sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  const orderedLegs=legs.slice().sort((a,b)=>M.cy(a)-M.cy(b));
  const thigh=orderedLegs.slice(0,2).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  const shin=orderedLegs.slice(2,4).sort((a,b)=>M.cx(a)-M.cx(b));
  return [head,torso,left[0],left[1],left[2],right[0],right[1],right[2],thigh[0],shin[0],thigh[1],shin[1]];
}

// New side-profile species sheets use the same 14 independent structural pieces as humanoids,
// but their natural silhouette is horizontal: head/front on the right, torso across the centre,
// six front-leg/wing pieces forward of the body centre, and six hind-leg/foot pieces toward the rear.
// The prompts intentionally place each limb chain in three vertical tiers (upper, lower, tip),
// which lets this geometric sorter map anatomy-position sheets without requiring a 27-cell grid.
function sheetOrderSpeciesAnatomy14(parts,w,h,modelType){
  if(!parts || parts.length!==14 || (modelType!=='animal' && modelType!=='bird')) return null;
  const M=sheetFigureMetrics(parts), cx=M.cx, cy=M.cy;
  const horizontal=parts.filter(b=>M.bw(b)>=M.w*.24 && M.bw(b)>M.bh(b)*1.25 && M.yr(b)>.16 && M.yr(b)<.78);
  if(!horizontal.length) return null;
  const torso=horizontal.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
    const score=x=>x.n*1.0 + M.bw(x)*.15 - Math.abs(cx(x)-(M.x0+M.w*.49))*.12 - Math.abs(cy(x)-(M.y0+M.h*.42))*.05;
    return score(b)-score(a);
  })[0];
  // The neutral head is deliberately positioned above and forward (right) of the body.
  // Prefer a compact right-side component just above the torso, rather than a wing/leg component.
  let headPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && cx(b)>cx(torso)+M.w*.08 && cy(b)<cy(torso)+M.h*.10 && M.yr(b)<.63);
  if(!headPool.length) headPool=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && cx(b)>cx(torso)+M.w*.10).sort((a,b)=>cy(a)-cy(b)).slice(0,3);
  if(!headPool.length) return null;
  const head=headPool.sort((a,b)=>{
    const score=x=> (cx(x)-cx(torso))*.75 + (cy(torso)-cy(x))*.35 + Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,x.n))*.025;
    return score(b)-score(a);
  })[0];
  const remaining=parts.filter(b=>b!==torso && b!==head);
  if(remaining.length!==12) return null;
  const byX=remaining.slice().sort((a,b)=>cx(a)-cx(b));
  const rear=byX.slice(0,6), forward=byX.slice(6);
